國際經濟金融週報內容摘要 
美 
國 
美國經濟維持穩健擴張，通膨壓力略升溫，Fed 維持利率不變，期中選舉進入
最後衝刺階段 
經濟 
⚫ 美國第三季實質 GDP 季增年率(初值)由上季的 2.2%升至 3.4%，成長主
要受民間消費 、 企業投資及庫存回補帶動 。 其中個人消費貢獻2.52 個百
分點、私人投資貢獻 3.27 個百分點，惟淨出口拖累 3.32 個百分點，整
體經濟仍維持穩健擴張。 
⚫ 9 月美國新屋銷售年化 67.6 萬戶，月減 0.6%、年減 5.3%，主要是中西
部及南部地區銷售下滑拖累整體表現。房價雖較上月回升，但仍低於去
年同期；供給月數 8.4 個月，顯示市場仍呈供給充裕、需求偏弱格局。 
⚫ 美國 9 月個人消費支出(PCE)物價指數年增率由 3.4%升至 3.6%，核心
PCE 物價指數年增率亦由 3.0%升至 3.1%，通膨再度升溫。儘管實質所
得受物價上漲影響而下滑，消費支出仍持續成長，反映家庭需求及內需
動能仍具韌性。 
⚫ 10 月 FOMC 會議以 10 比 2 決議將聯邦基金利率目標區間維持於 3.75%
至 4.0%。決策聲明指出，美國經濟活動表現強勁，就業市場變化不大，
通膨持續高於 2%目標，將依後續數據評估適當政策立場，以確保通膨
回歸目標。兩名委員主張升息 1 碼，顯示內部對通膨風險仍存分歧。主
席 Kevin Warsh 強調將防止能源價格上漲擴散至整體物價 ， 並宣布2027
年起部分 FOMC 會議改為策略討論會議，以強化長期政策研議。 
重要 
事件 
⚫ 10 月 26 日路透社民調顯示，川普施政支持率 32%，略高於前週；國會
選舉政黨支持度則由民主黨 47%領先共和黨 36%。截至 10 月 31 日，
提前及郵寄投票已逾 5,100 萬人。整體而言，期中選舉結果將影響未來
國會版圖、施政推動及貿易政策方向，備受市場關注。 
短評 
⚫ 美國第三季 GDP 成長優於前季，主要受民間消費、企業投資及庫存回
補帶動，顯示內需與企業活動仍具韌性；惟新屋銷售持續疲弱、房市需
求未明顯改善，反映高利率環境仍抑制部分經濟活動。另一方面，PCE
通膨再度升溫，核心通膨降幅有限，顯示物價壓力仍存。在經濟穩健且
通膨高於目標情況下，Fed 維持利率不變並釋出偏鷹訊號，後續貨幣政
策料將維持審慎，持續關注通膨與就業數據變化。

但隨通膨風險持續上行，後續仍具升息空間，貨幣政策可望維持漸進緊

國

短評 ⚫ 工業部門營收受到物價上漲帶動，但成本轉嫁能力有限，反而降低利潤表現。

增速料將放緩，後續仍須關注 AI 需求延續性、成長動能向傳統產業擴

兆豐國際商業銀行 徵信處 
115.11.2 
一、 美國—經濟維持穩健擴張，通膨壓力略升溫，Fed 維持利率不變，期中選
舉進入最後衝刺階段 
2026 年第三季美國實質國內生產毛額(GDP)季增年率初值為 3.4%， 高於第二
季之 2.2%，整體景氣持續穩健擴張。就成長貢獻而言，個人消費支出仍為經濟成
長主要支柱，貢獻 2.52 個百分點，其中服務消費及商品消費分別貢獻 1.89 個百
分點及 0.63 個百分點 ， 反映民間需求維持穩健 ； 私人投資則貢獻3.27 個百分點 ，
為本季成長最大亮點，其中固定投資貢獻1.66 個百分點，而企業庫存回補亦額外
貢獻 1.61 個百分點。對外貿易方面，淨出口拖累經濟成長 3.32 個百分點，其中
出口減少造成 0.68 個百分點負面影響；進口增加則拖累 2.64 個百分點。政府消
費支出與投資則受聯邦政府非國防支出增加帶動，貢獻 0.90 個百分點。此外，第
三季個人消費支出(PCE)物價指數及核心 PCE 物價指數分別年增 2.1%及 2.5%，
均低於第二季水準。整體而言，美國第三季經濟成長主要受民間消費、企業投資
及庫存回補支撐 ， 雖貿易逆差擴大對GDP 形成明顯拖累 ， 但內需表現仍具韌性 ，
顯示美國經濟基本面維持穩健。 
2026 年 9 月美國新屋銷售經季節性調整後年化戶數為 67.6 萬戶，較上月減
少 0.6%， 並較去年同期下降5.3%， 顯示在高利率環境下住宅需求仍較去年疲弱 。
地區別表現分歧，其中東北部及西部分別較上月增加 28.0%及 21.4%，惟中西部
及南部分別減少 7.3%及 6.6%，尤其占全美銷售比重最大的南部地區回落，成為
拖累整體銷售的主因。價格方面，9 月新屋銷售中位價為 40.04 萬美元，較上月
上升 3.9%，但較去年同期下跌 3.9%，儘管房價高於上月，惟仍面臨修正壓力。
供給方面，9 月底待售新屋數量為 47.6 萬戶，較上月減少1.0%，較去年同期減少
2.3%；按目前銷售速度計算，供給月數為 8.4 個月，低於上月的 8.5 個月，但高
於去年同期的 8.2 個月。整體而言，9 月美國新屋銷售較前月小幅下滑且年減幅
度擴大，反映購屋需求仍未明顯改善；雖然部分地區銷售回升且庫存略有下降，
但供給月數仍維持相對偏高水準，顯示新屋市場仍處於供給較充裕、需求偏弱的
格局，後續仍須觀察利率走勢及就業市場變化對住宅需求的影響。 
2026 年 9 月美國個人消費支出(PCE)物價指數月增 0.5%， 高於8 月之 0.3%，

年增率則由 3.4%升至 3.6%； 扣除食品及能源後之核心PCE 月增率維持於 0.3%，
年增率由 3.0%微升至 3.1%，能源價格回升及服務價格上漲持續對整體物價形成
支撐，通膨再度升溫。另一方面，個人所得月增 0.2%，可支配所得(DPI)亦月增
0.2%，個人消費支出則月增 0.5%，增幅高於所得成長，顯示家庭消費需求仍具韌
性；惟受物價上漲影響，實質可支配所得月減0.3%，實質個人消費支出則持平，
反映名目支出增加部分已被物價上升所抵銷。整體而言，美國 9 月消費活動維持
穩健，內需仍為經濟成長提供支撐，但通膨壓力較前月略有升溫，物價回落至聯
準會 2%目標之過程仍具挑戰，後續仍須持續關注能源價格變化、核心通膨走勢
及消費動能對聯準會貨幣政策方向之影響。 
聯準會(Fed)於 2026 年 10 月 FOMC 會議以 10 比 2 表決結果，決定將聯邦基
金利率目標區間維持於 3.75%至 4.00%不變，符合市場預期；謹將本次會議聲明
及主席 Kevin Warsh 記者會重點摘錄如下：(一)聯準會認為美國經濟活動持續以
強勁步調擴張，較前次聲明所稱之「穩健擴張」進一步上修對經濟表現之評價；
儘管地緣政治情勢使經濟前景不確定性仍偏高，但國內支出維持韌性，生產力成
長與資本投資表現強勁，就業增幅大致與勞動力成長同步，失業率變動不大，顯
示整體經濟基本面仍具相當韌性；(二)通膨仍高於聯準會 2%目標，委員會表示需
累積更多經濟與物價資訊，以評估適當政策立場，確保通膨適時回歸 2%目標，
並重申將致力維持物價穩定 ；(三)本次決議出現兩張反對票 ， 聯準會理事Lisa Cook
及克里夫蘭聯邦準備銀行總裁 Beth Hammack 主張升息 1 碼，反映部分委員對通
膨風險仍抱持較為謹慎態度；(四)主席 Kevin Warsh 於記者會表示，近期聯邦公
開市場委員會討論焦點多集中於物價穩定，特別是在近期能源及相關價格再度上
揚情況下，聯準會有責任避免個別產業價格上漲擴散至整體經濟，並確保通膨預
期維持穩定；(五)Warsh 並宣布調整 FOMC 會議安排，2027 年仍將維持每年 8 次
會議，但其中 1 次將改為專門討論貨幣政策策略之會議，不進行利率表決或發布
政策聲明，並預期 2028 年將有 2 次會議採取此模式，以騰出更多時間進行長期
政策與重大議題研議。整體而言，本次會議雖維持利率不變，但聯準會仍認為通
膨問題尚未完全解決，後續政策方向仍將取決於通膨、勞動市場及整體經濟數據
表現。 
根據 10 月 26 日路透社最新民調，總統川普施政支持率為 32%，較前一週之
31%略為回升；在國會選舉政黨支持度方面， 47%的登記選民表示傾向支持民主

支持度上仍具優勢，但相關結果僅反映調查期間之民意分布，尚難直接推論最終

州的選務程序、郵寄選票資格認定及選舉公正性相關爭議亦持續受到輿論關注。
整體而言，本次期中選舉結果除將影響國會兩院席次分布外，亦可能牽動川普政
府後續施政推動、預算協商及貿易政策方向，受到國際市場高度關注。 
二、歐元區—歐洲央行如期暫停升息，12 月升息機率較高 
 歐元區第三季 GDP 季增率為 0.4%，但有部份是受到愛爾蘭經濟成長率大幅
波動的扭曲，若剔除愛爾蘭，則歐元區第三季 GDP 季增率僅約 0.2%。該季受到
物價壓力上升 、 地緣政治緊張局勢等因素影響 ， 對消費及投資需求構成負面因素 ，
但世足賽以及德國財政支出擴張的支持下，仍使歐元區經濟保持正向表現。就國
別而言，德、法、義、西GDP 季增率分別為 1.2%、-0.1%、0.8%、2.7%；其中德
國經濟活動受到財政擴張的提振；法國的消費支出雖強勁，但受到投資減緩和進
口增加拖累；義大利製造業產出萎縮拖累成長表現；西班牙在住宅營建熱潮的支
持下， 經濟活動持續優於其他國家， 但歐盟NGEU 計畫到期以及政府財政整頓力
道，西班牙後續經濟動能可能會逐漸放緩。 
 歐洲央行在 10 月 29 日宣布維持政策利率不變，主要是近期歐元區各國殖利
率普遍上升 ， 對經濟造成限制性因素 ， 且不確定性可能會隨著時間的推移而累積 ，
從而拖累經濟成長。不過，歐洲央行仍認為近期高漲的能源價格對通膨的壓力相
當大，使央行考慮進一步採取貨幣政策措施因應。因此，除非經濟成長受到超出
預期的衝擊、能源價格下跌或金融環境急劇收緊，一般預期歐洲央行在 12 月的
政策會議將會宣布升息一碼。 
三、日本—日本央行暫停升息，通膨壓力續存，升息預期升溫 
 9 月工業生產月增 2.2%，扭轉 8 月受天災造成生產中斷而下滑的情勢，從細
項來看，汽車、電子產品及生產機械產量均呈回升，其中生產機械已連續第四個
月成長，反映 AI 相關資本支出需求持續暢旺；庫存月增 2.5%，石化產品庫存亦
逐步回補。展望未來，製造商預估 10 月及 11 月產量將分別月增 5.1%及 0.8%，

用相關內容。

7

顯示在 AI 及資本支出需求、汽車復產與庫存回補支撐下，短期工業生產動能可
望維持強勁。 
 9 月零售月減 0.7%，主要受雨季抑制家庭外出消費，以及先前受新規上路前
換機需求及汽車稅減免政策帶動的耐久財消費動能逐漸減弱，致整體消費動能偏
弱；惟服裝及一般商品銷售受假期延長與節慶需求支撐而有所回升。另 10 月消
費者信心指數下降 0.4 點至 35.0，連續第二個月下滑，反映消費者對物價上漲及
實質購買力下降的憂慮加深，預期未來一年通膨率超過 2%的消費者比重亦升至
79.8%。 展望未來， 隨天候等暫時性不利因素逐漸消退 ，加上所得成長仍具韌性，
第四季消費動能可望回升；惟通膨持續升溫，加上汽油補貼逐步退場，恐進一步
侵蝕購買力，為消費復甦的下行風險。 
 10 月東京核心 CPI 年增 2.6%，石油相關成本上漲已由核心商品逐步擴散至服
務價格，食品及生鮮食品價格亦偏強。雖目前能源、汽油補貼等政府措施仍抑制
通膨 ， 但隨12 月相關補貼逐步退場 ， 成本壓力將進一步反映於商品及服務價格 ，
預期至 2026 年底潛在通膨仍可能高於央行的 2%目標，通膨黏性值得持續關注。 
 日本央行於 10 月 29、30 日召開貨幣政策會議 ， 維持政策利率於1.25%不變，
主要係仍需觀察先前升息的累積效果 ， 但整體政策訊號仍偏鷹派 。 本次決議以8：
1 通過，唯一反對的政策委員高田創主張再升息 1 碼至 1.5%，反映央行內部對通
膨上行風險仍具警戒 。 日本央行指出通膨已大致達到與 2%通膨目標相符的水準 ，
同時將 2027 財年的核心 CPI(不含生鮮食品)預測由 2.4%上調至 2.6%，顯示目前
通膨情勢仍支持貨幣政策進一步正常化 。央行總裁表示 ， 目前金融環境仍屬寬鬆 ，
未來若經濟及物價走勢符合預期，仍將適時調升政策利率，市場因而持續關注後
續會議再度升息的可能性。此外，日本央行亦決定自 2027 年 1 月起，將 ETF 出
售規模由約 3,300 億日圓提高至 8,600 億日圓，加速資產負債表正常化進程。 
四、中國—工業部門成本轉嫁能力有限，使利潤率持續萎縮 
1-9 月工業部門營業收入、利潤年增率分別為 15.5、13.1%，其中營業收入年
增率較上期(1-8 月)大增 8.9 個百分點，但利潤年增率下降 4.5 個百分點。受惠於
金屬、塑化原物料上漲，加上 AI 需求擴張，以及政府推動高科技產業發展等因
素，營收成長貢獻度較高者包含有色金屬加工(營收、利潤年增 17.5%、79.7%)、
計算機、通信和其他電子設備製造業(營收、利潤年增 21%、103.8%)、化學原料

及製品(營收、利潤年增 9.4%、49.2%)。此外，電氣機械及器材業、汽車業營收年
增率雖分別為 11.3%、2.6%，但獲利分別年減 5.9%、22.8%。非金屬礦物製品業
營收、獲利下滑9.7、46.9%，黑色金屬加工業營收、獲利分別年減1.5%、73.3%，
反映產業持續進行減產所致。整體而言，工業部門營收受到物價上漲帶動，但成
本轉嫁能力有限，反而降低利潤表現；部分產業受惠於短期原物料價格波動，以
及受到政策推動的高科技產業表現較佳，惟部分產業因陷入產能調整週期，或政
府減少支持等因素而表現較弱，產業分化明顯。 
五、印度及東南亞—泰、菲進出口創歷史新高，泰國央行按兵不動以支撐經濟 
(1) 新加坡：9 月消費者物價指數(CPI)年增率小幅回落至 2.2%、核心通膨率自 8
月的 2.2%降至 2%，反映交通運輸（7.4%） 、服裝鞋帽（2.1%）及家庭耐用品
和服務 （0.2%）價格漲幅顯著放緩，此外，由於行業競爭壓力，資訊通信技術
服務價格的進一步回落也抑制整體服務價格的上漲。隨著 11 月電力和天然氣
價格下調生效，預計 2026 年第四季核心通膨將進一步放緩。 
(2) 泰國：9 月出口額達 384.4 億美元、年增 24.0%，創歷史新高，主要得益於工
業產品 出口成長 25.4%，其中， 以電腦及相關設備（ 59.4%）、電話及零件
（117.5%）及電路板（45.9%）成長最為顯著，對美國出口額年增 50.1%，對
中國和日本的出口額分別成長 18.4%和 19.0%。9 月進口額 443 億美元、年增
50.4%，創歷史新高，因出口導向製造業生產需求及全球能源價格的上漲，主
要類別進口額均有所成長，其中，資本貨物(電腦、機械零件、電路板等)進口
大增 92.3%，燃料、原材料和中間產品進口增幅均逾 4 成、運輸設備在飛機與
航空設備進口大增 12 倍下成長 24%。9 月貿易逆差大幅擴大至 59 億美元，累
計 1 至 9 月 ， 貿易逆差達 437 億美元 ， 出口額成長19.6%、進口額成長 37.8%。
泰國央行 10 月會議維持基準利率於 1%不變，維持寬鬆的貨幣政策立場，以
支持經濟復甦的不平衡 。經濟預估方面 ，強於預期的出口以及與科技和人工智
慧週期相關的投資，預估 2026 年和 2027 年經濟成長率將分別成長 2.4%和
2.1%，然而，高額的家庭債務、脆弱的勞動市場和疲軟的購買力繼續抑制私人
消費，使經濟成長仍低於潛在水平。通膨方面，受能源價格上漲和聖嬰現象的
影響，預計通膨率在 2027 年初將出現短暫上升，之後隨著國內需求的疲軟而
回落，2026 年和 2027 年整體通膨率平均分別為 2.1%和 1.3%。

(3) 菲律賓:9 月出口額達 98.7 億美元、 年增35.6%，主要由電子產品出口成長 72%
所帶動，占總出口額達 70.1%，其中半導體元件、辦公設備、電子設備及零件
出口成長分別達 86%、89%、73.5%，對美國出口大增 135%(26.3 億美元、占
總出口 26.6%)，對台灣出口成長 36.8%，對越南、新加坡分別成長 488%及
30%。9 月進口額達 150.3 億美元、年增 25.7%，創歷史新高，主要係電子產
品進口成長 87.4%(半導體 144%)，礦物燃料和潤滑油 、 穀物及穀物製品進口亦
分別成長 53.3%、22.8%，韓國是主要進口來源國、占進口總額 26.1%，其次
為中國占 22.3%。進出口相抵，9 月貿易逆差擴大至 51.5 億美元，累計 1 至 9
月 ， 貿易逆差從去年同期的376 億美元擴大至 472 億美元，出口額成長 17.2%、
進口額成長 20.3%。 
六、台灣—AI 動能續強，經濟成長動能穩健 
9 月景氣對策信號續呈紅燈，綜合判斷分數為 41 分，較上月增加 1 分，燈號
續呈紅燈，其中工業及服務業加班工時，受惠 AI 相關產業生產動能維持熱絡，
由綠燈轉呈黃紅燈，其餘八項燈號均維持不變。另領先與同時指標皆呈上升，顯
示國內經濟動能維持穩健。 
主計總處 10 月 30 日公布台灣第三季經濟成長率為 14.36%，主要受 AI 需求
強勁帶動出口及企業投資大幅成長，民間消費亦維持熱絡，顯示外需動能正逐步
向內需擴散，整體經濟呈現內外皆熱。展望未來，AI 需求、半導體相關投資及企
業資本支出持續擴張，加上年底消費旺季，可望支撐短期景氣表現；惟今年經濟
高速成長墊高比較基期，明年增速料將放緩，後續仍須關注 AI 需求延續性、成
長動能向傳統產業擴散情形及金融市場波動等不確定因素。

Powertech Technology
Subsidiaries drive stronger revenue/OP growth;
FOPLP expansion back on track
Q326 results broadly in-line despite better GM
Q326 EPS of NT$3.54 was broadly in-line with UBSe/consensus as stronger  revenue and
better GM were offset by higher tax and minority interests. Q326 sales rose 9.6% QoQ
to NT$25.3 bn on robust DRAM/eSSD demand and price hikes, ahead of UBSe/Street's
+5-6% QoQ.  GM expanded by 2.2ppt QoQ to 24.0%, supported by better pricing,

higher loadings and more favourable  mix. By business, higher GM testing  increased by
1ppt QoQ to 24% of total sales, while lower margin SiP/module declined 2ppt QoQ to
9%, and packaging  increased 1ppt QoQ to 67%. By entity, we estimate 43%-owned
Greatek  and 61%-owned Teraprobe  each delivered mid-teens % QoQ revenue growth
in Q326, versus  only mid-single % growth at PTI standalone. The stronger contribution
from subsidiaries, however, resulted in higher effective tax rate and minority interests.

Near-term outlook  remains constructive
Management remains constructive on the near-term outlook, supported by AI/cloud
investment cycle, continued memory supply constraint, and PTI's ongoing diversification
into non-memory segments. Q426F UTRs are expected to remain elevated following
over-90% levels in Q3, while ASPs should continue to increase as customers absorb
higher raw material costs. PTI is also expanding its exposure to logic Flip-Chip BGA, while

premium-segment automotive demand remains stable. Nevertheless, consumer
electronics demand is showing some weakness amid higher memory pricing. We
forecast Q426 sales  to grow 4% QoQ and  GM to expand  0.8ppt QoQ to 24.8%.
Looking into 2027E, we believe  GM to remain in the mid-20% range, as continued
memory supply tightness and  better mix should largely offset higher  depreciation.
FOPLP expansion back on track
PTI indicated its FOPLP expansion remains on track for mass production in 2027, with the

equipment delivery schedule having improved. It sees robust customer demand  with
notable increases in New Product Introduction (NPI). We continue to model FOPLP
capacity of 3K substrates/month in 2026E, rising to 6K by mid-2027E in Taiwan, with
panel substrate size of 510 mm x 515 mm. Importantly, PTI  aims to serve end-customers
directly and capture greater value-add, rather than operate  as an outsourcing partner to
leading wafer foundry. Its JV with Broadcom remains  under review by Taiwan's MOEA

Investment Commission with management expecting a decision in the  near-term.
Valuation: Raise PT to NT$280, retain NEUTRAL
We revise our 2026/27/28E EPS by 3%/9%/7% on better sales/GM, offset by higher tax
and minority interest from subsidiaries. We raise PT to NT$280 (from NT$260) with the
same 17x 2027E PE. While near-term outlook has improved we believe further upside
will depend on the execution of the  FOPLP ramp and the sustainability of pricing

by the analyst to indicate that the stock's price target and/or rating may change in the near term, usually in response to an event that

may affect the investment case or valuation. Equity Price Targets have a 12-month investment horizon.

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.

Taiwan Semis: GUC (3443.TW): Mgmt. Call takeaways: Server CPU ramp 
to sustain momentum into 2027; opportunities across applications
31 October 2026 | 12:35AM HKT
We talked to GUC (3443.TW, not covered) management recently. The company’s Sep 
revenues were up 22% MoM (vs. +1% MoM in Aug), reflecting its revenues from CPU 
of a leading US CSP in strong ramp up with more foundry capacity rolling out, and 
would continue to support the growth in 4Q26 and 2027 per the company. The 
positive tone echoes our positive view on Global Servers TAM, which we expect 
leading US cloud service providers (CSPs) in aggregate will grow their capex budgets 
by 84%/ 52%/ 13% YoY in 2026-28E, with our estimates being raised by 4% / 17% / 
22% in 2026-28E to US$1,397bn by 2028E. Revenues in 3Q26 are driven by both 
NRE and turnkey solutions, with turnkey solutions revenues up 44% QoQ on the CPU 
ramp up, and despite turnkey solutions up to ~90% of 3Q26 revenues, the GM 
sustained at 20%+ in 3Q26, thanks to higher-margin NRE milestones. GUC adjusts 
pricing to reflect rising wafer and memory costs, and management sees memory 
impact is manageable on its customers’ projects. On competition, management 
highlighted its position as a core partner of TSMC, with in-house IP for 3D stacking as 
a key differentiator. GUC’s strong turnkey pull-in from its CSP customer’s server CPU 
and its focus on IP for TSMC 3D stacking point to continued demand for TSMC’s 
(2330.TW, Buy) advanced nodes and packaging. The positive tone on ASIC demand 
also echoes our positive view on rising ASIC penetration rate (~60% by 2028E, report 
link), supported by leading CSPs diversifying thier AI chips platforms and reducing 
token per second per costs, driving inferencing / training efficiency.

Company profile: GUC (3443.TW, not covered) is a ASIC design service company 
and a TSMC affiliate, providing NRE design services and turnkey production for

### Page 2

custom chips manufactured at TSMC. Its customers span cloud (CPUs, AI accelerators,
BMCs), automotive and consumer (image sensors, SSD controllers), with 68% of its
products under 7nm.
Key takeaways
1. Mass production as the key profit driver: Management noted that the key to
profitability lies in converting NRE projects into mass production. With the largest
customer’s CPU now in volume production, turnkey has become the main growth driver.
NRE carries higher GM than turnkey, but as it is recognized by milestone, quarterly NRE

margin varies with the mix of projects at different stages.
2. Expanding opportunities across cloud and automotive: GUC continues to open up
new opportunities across applications. In cloud, it works with all major US CSPs on
projects spanning CPUs and GPUs and also supplies other server projects such as BMCs,
while actively pursuing further AI accelerator opportunities. In automotive, it is engaged
with both US and Chinese customers at the NRE stage, two of which already plan for

mass production.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.
Coverage Universe: A list of all stocks in each coverage universe is available by primary analyst, stock and coverage universe at

https://www.gs.com/research/hedge.html.

Lite-On Tech

Upgrade to Buy; 3Q26 beat, better margin; 
solid AI outlook; lift PO to NT$288

Rating Change: BUY | PO: 288.00 TWD | Price: 240.50 TWD

Upgrade to Buy and raise PO to NT$288  
We upgrade Lite-On Tech (LoT) to Buy from Neutral with a higher PO of NT$288 (18x 
2027E EPS; prior NT$285 with valuation unchanged). We turn more positive on LoT 
after two straight quarters of results beat, especially with margin surprise. We lift 2026-
28E EPS by 1-8% (Exhibit 1), with still solid AI power-supply demand/order. Our 2026-
28E EPS is 4-7% above consensus (Exhibit 2). We expect the market to react more 
positively to LoT, with 3Q26 results beat, vs. Delta with 3Q26 results miss (see Robert 
Cheng’s Delta 3Q26 note), amid similar positive comments on long-term AI market 
demand. 
3Q26 beat: better margins, despite lighter sales 
LoT’s 3Q26 GM/OPM of 25.9%/15.1% came in better than BofAe of 25.3%/13.5% and 
consensus of 25.1%/13.6%, despite lighter sales (3%/5% below BofAe/consensus). We 
view the QoQ GM/OPM decline from record high in 2Q26 as largely expected as mgmt. 
had indicated at 2Q26 meeting that 1H26 GM of 24.7% represented a more sustainable 
level. Besides AI, better margins were also helped by improving product mix in IT/CE (i.e. 
higher mix from high-end ICT and LEO satellite) and higher automation level.  
4Q26 sales up QoQ; 2H26 GM above 1H; rising AI mix 
Similar to Delta, LoT also sees short-term shipment uncertainties/push-outs due to 
component shortage and power/electricity shortage for AI datacenter infrastructure. 
Nevertheless, the CEO remains long-term positive on AI demand. He also expects easing 
component/power constraints from 4Q26. Yet, mgmt. expects 4Q26 cloud sales to grow 
QoQ/YoY, led by ASIC/GPU/AMD projects, and 2H26 GM to be above 1H26 level (BofAe: 
26.3% in 4Q26, vs. 25.9%/24.7% in 3Q26/1H26). The CEO indicated 800VDC power rack 
mass shipment in 1Q27, after sample shipment in 4Q26. Mgmt. expects AI sales mix at 
30%+ in 2026 and 40-50% in 2027. This time, LoT raised 2026 capex to NT$19.7bn 
(prior NT$18.0bn) and sees higher level in 2027 (BofAe: NT$20.0bn).

Key Metrics      
* For full definitions of iQmethod SM measures, see page 9.

Company Sector 
Electronics Manufacturing Services

Company Description 
Lite-On Tech is a leading PC and server power-supply maker 
globally. Besides PC/server power-supply, its key products 
include optoelectronics (photo-coupler, LED lighting, 
outdoor lighting, auto lighting) and power-supply for game 
console and industrial. Key applications include AIoT, 5G, 
and smart life. Its key customers include HP, Dell, Lenovo, 
Microsoft, Sony, Nintendo, and global cloud hyperscalers. 
Lite-On Tech was founded in 1975 and headquartered in 
Taiwan.

Investment Rationale 
We have a Buy rating on Lite-On Tech in view of (1) its solid 
position in AI server power-supply as the second largest 
component maker globally and (2) expanding margins with 
improving product mix, rising scale, and better operating 
efficiency. We see consistently high cash dividend payout 
(over 50% since 2008) and solid balance sheet as share 
price downside supports.

Upgrade to Buy; raise PO to NT$288  
We upgrade Lite-On Tech (LoT) to Buy, from Neutral, with a higher PO of NT$288 (18x 
2027E EPS) from NT$285 with valuation unchanged. We turn more positive on LoT after 
two straight quarters of results beat, especially with margin surprise. We lift 2026-28E 
EPS by 1-8% (Exhibit 1) to reflect 3Q26 results beat, the latest guidance, and solid AI 
power-supply demand/order. Our 2026-28E EPS is 4-7% above consensus (Exhibit 2).  
LoT’s share price corrected 21% and 14% in the recent three months and one month, 
respectively, vs. key peer Delta’s (2308 TT; covered by Robert Cheng) -27%/-29% and 
TAIEX’s -2%/-6%. We expect more positive market reaction to LoT’s 3Q26 results beat, 
vs. Delta’s 3Q26 results miss (see Robert Cheng’s Delta 3Q26 note), amid similar 
positive comments on long-term AI market demand. LoT also has a cheaper valuation vs. 
Delta. LoT currently trades at 15x 2027E P/E, below Delta’s 24x and overall peers’ 
average of 20x (Exhibit 6).

3Q26 beat: better margins, despite lighter sales 
LoT posted 3Q26 results and held the physical analyst meeting on 30 Oct after market 
close. 3Q26 GM/OPM of 25.9%/15.1% came in better than BofAe of 25.3%/13.5% and 
consensus of 25.1%/13.6%, despite lighter sales (3%/5% below BofAe/consensus; +9% 
QoQ, +28% YoY). We view the QoQ decline in GM/OPM from the record highs in 2Q26

### Page 4

as largely expected, as management had already indicated at the 2Q26 meeting that the 
1H26 GM of 24.7% represented a more sustainable level (see our 2Q26 result note). 
Besides AI, we believe better margins were also helped by improving product mix in 
IT/CE (i.e. higher mix from high-end ICT and LEO satellite) and higher automation level. 
With non-operating gain of NT$1.1bn (including net interest income of NT$369mn and 
FX gain of NT$734mn) and higher minority interest, net income (+4% QoQ, +60% YoY) 
was 12%/11% above BofAe/consensus.

BofA GLOBAL RESEARCH

Similar comments on material/power shortage like Delta 
Similar to Delta mgmt.’s comments at 3Q26 analyst meeting, LoT also sees short-term 
shipment uncertainties/push-outs due to material/component shortage (e.g. semis, PCB, 
passives) and power/electricity shortage for AI datacenter infrastructure. Nevertheless, 
LoT’s CEO remains long-term positive on AI demand with ongoing platform/spec 
upgrade. He also expects easing component/power constraints from 4Q26, with ongoing 
strategic component procurement/preparation and customers’ robust AI demand.

4Q26 sales up QoQ; 2H26 GM above 1H; rising AI mix 
Into 4Q26, mgmt. expects cloud sales to grow both QoQ and YoY. Key drivers include (1) 
volume production of 8.5kW PSUs for ASIC applications and BBUs, next-generation 
Nvidia Vera Rubin 110kW solutions, 42-inch power shelves for AMD Helios, (2) prototype 
shipments of 800VDC power rack, and (3) continued momentum in high-end power 
applications, with shipments of power solutions for LEO satellites. the CEO also expects 
2H26 GM to be above 1H26 level. We thus project 4Q26 overall sales to grow 5% QoQ 
(+36% YoY), with GM at 26.3% (vs. 25.9%/24.7% in 3Q26/1H26). For HVDC, the CEO 
further stated 800VDC power rack mass shipment in 1Q27, after final validation and 
sample shipment in 4Q26. Mgmt. kept AI sales mix target at 30%+ in 2026 and 
potentially 40-50% in 2027. LoT raised 2026 capex to NT$19.7bn (vs. NT$18.0bn guided 
at 2Q26 analyst meeting) and expects a higher level in 2027 (BofAe: NT$20.0bn).

Initially expects 2027 sales/GM/OPM YoY increase 
Into 2027, the CEO initially expects cloud sales to grow another 70% YoY, driven by 
rising ASP and power-supply product/spec upgrade. He targets another 10% YoY sales 
growth for optoelectronics business, driven by industrial and new energy business. He 
expects LEO satellite power to grow YoY but sees flattish sales from conventional ICT 
power business. Nevertheless, he expects GM/OPM YoY expansion in 2027. We thus 
expect 2027 sales to grow 38% YoY, with GM/OPM increasing by 1.6ppt/1.5ppt YoY.

### Page 7

Price objective basis & risk 
Delta Electronics Inc. (DLTEF) 
Our PO of NT$2,250 is based on 30x 4Q27-3Q28E EPS, which is close to 2.0 standard 
deviations above Delta's average trading P/E since 2012. We believe the P/E multiple is 
justified, given (1) continuous strength and leading position in server power-supply and 
power choke business, (2) content/value growth with broader product offering in AI 
(artificial intelligence) business, (3) rising cooling sales by targeting top CSPs' 
customized projects, and (4) ongoing margin expansion.

The upside risks to our PO are (1) stronger-than-expected server/datacenter demand, (2) 
faster-than-expected IA recovery, and (3) faster-than-expected EV model launches by 
global brands.

The downside risks to our PO are (1) unexpected changes in policy that could impact IA 
growth in China, (2) slower-than-expected server/datacenter demand recovery, (3) 
earnings impact from unprofitable long-term investments (4) Adverse effects of 
announced USITC 337 investigation into multiple companies in the AI supply chain, 
which includes Delta Electric. 
Lite-On Tech (LOTZF) 
Our PO of NT$288 is based on 18x 2027E EPS, which is around 1.75SD above its 
historical average since 2013. We believe the P/E multiple is justified, given its solid 
market position to capture multi-year AI server power-supply market demand with 
improving product mix. Yet, we also assign a valuation discount to its key peer, given its 
late entry and a slower progress in cooling business, smaller scale, and lighter margin 
profile.

Downside risks: (1) weaker-than-expected demand from server/cloud power-supply 
business, (2) lighter margin profile for automotive LED lighting with limited ASP 
expansion, (3) pricing competition and component price increase from power-supply 
business, and (4) less order win from cooling business.

Upside risks: (1) stronger-than-expected margin profile for automotive LED lighting with 
ASP expansion, (2) faster revenue growth from server/cloud power-supply with a more 
positive datacenter market outlook, and (3) larger/stronger order win from cooling 
business.

長榮航太 (2645 TT)  Trading Buy

預期波音 737 系列交機速度將略為放緩。  
投資建議 ：雖然 2026 年長榮航太的維修業務持續擴張，但考量
波音將維持 737 系列月均交機一段時間，可能會影響長榮航太
的製造部門復甦情形，故給予長榮航太 Trading Buy 的投資建
議。  
波音 737 系列月交機將維持一段時間，調整長榮航太後續製造
的營運預估： 波音於 10/27/2026 公告 3Q26 財報，並於法說會
上提及 :「當前需維持 737 系列月均交機量穩定在 47 架一段時
間，後續管理階層預期交機提升可能要到 2H27 才會上升至 52
架」，使得長榮航太的製造部門復甦仍需等待一段時間，故研
究部一併調整長榮航太後續的營運預估。  
3Q26 先前的維修漲價陸續貢獻，營運維持高檔： 3Q26 長榮航
太自 2H24 起陸續針對維修業務進行漲價後，加上全球機隊持
續供不應求，增加維修機率與維修產值，惟製造部門雖然波音
已將 737 系列交機上升至 47 架 /月，但短期拉貨力道仍有限，
使得長榮航太的製造業務與轉投資公司營運維持低檔，營運仍
以維修業務為主， 3Q26 營收 52.75 億元， QoQ+1.02% ，預估稅
後純益 8.31 億元， QoQ+10.34% ， EPS 2.22 元。  
2027 年空中巴士首件交付、無人機新訂單，有望成為中長期營
運新動能： 2027 年長榮航太除了持續受惠維修業務與波音交機
的逐年擴張之外，在新訂單的部分，先前與空中巴士的新訂單，
預估 1Q27 將迎來首件交付，並待後續取得特殊製程認證後，
2H27 有望迎來新訂單的量產，以及無人機訂單也有望成為長榮
航太新一波的營運動能，預估 2027 年營收 224.86 億元，
YoY+7.85% ，稅後純益 37.61 億元， YoY+17.06% ，EPS 10.04 元。

3 群益投顧 
圖一、全球商用飛機平均機齡

資料來源 : IATA ，長榮航太  
波音 737 系列月交機將維持一段時間，調整長榮航太後續製造的營運預估：
波音於 10/27/2026 公告 3Q26 財報，並於法說會上提及 :「當前需維持 737 系
列月均交機量穩定在 47 架一段時間，後續管理階層預期交機提升可能要到
2H27 才會上升至 52 架」，使得長榮航太的製造部門復甦仍需等待一段時
間，故研究部一併調整長榮航太後續的營運預估。  
2027 年空中巴士首件交付、無人機新訂單，有望成為中長期營運新動能：  
2027 年長榮航太除了持續受惠維修業務與波音交機的逐年擴張之外，在新
訂單的部分，先前與空中巴士的新訂單，預估 1Q27 將迎來首件交付，並待
後續取得特殊製程認證後， 2H27 有望迎來新訂單的量產，以及無人機訂單
也有望成為長榮航太新一波的營運動能，預估 2027 年營收 224.86 億元，
YoY+7.85% ，稅後純益 37.61 億元， YoY+17.06% ， EPS 10.04 元。  
投資建議：  
雖然 2026 年長榮航太的維修業務持續擴張，但考量波音將維持 737 系列月
均交機一段時間，可能會影響長榮航太的製造部門復甦情形，故給予長榮航
太 Trading Buy 的投資建議。

7 群益投顧 
  【投資評等說明】  
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首次評等潛在上漲空間 ≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy) 5% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無法由基本面給予投資評等  
預期近期股價將處於盤整  
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】  
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。   
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。  
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」 、 「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。  
停止推薦情境：  
1.  達目標價。  
2.  雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。  
3.  推薦後股價不漲反跌。

【免責聲明】  
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」 。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

주가 추이

견조한 판매 확대로 매출 견조, 이익은 시장 기대치 하회 예상 
3Q26 동사 매출액과 영업이익은 각각 33.6조원(+17.3% 이하 Y oY)과 2.39조
원(+63.6%) 기록 전망. 3Q 매출액은 친환경차 중심 신차 확대 통해 두 자릿수 
성장세를 기록. 3Q 판매량은 도매 기준 84.3만대(+7.6% 이하 Y oY)로 전년 동
기 대비 성장 폭을 확대했고, 특히 친환경차 판매량 29.8만대(+45.9%)를 기록
하며 이번 분기 매출 성장에 절대적으로 기여.  
다만, 3Q 수익성은 4~5월 상승했던 원자재 투입 영향과 ‘경제형EV 믹스’가 더
욱 확대(13.2% → 14.5%)되면서 단기 수익성에 부정적 작용하며 시장 기대치 
2.5조원을 다소 하회할 전망. 그러나, 기말 환율이 1,356원/$ 수준으로 낮 아 지
면서 전년 동기 수 익 성 악화에 크게 영 향 을 미쳤던 판매보증비 외화환산손익 
영향이 개선(1.6%p 수준) 기대되며, 관세 부담 또한 북미 HEV 확대로 전년 동
기 대비 약 4,500억원(2.0%p) 감소하면서 부정적 수익성 영향 만회 전망.  
유럽은 점유율 확대, 북미는 수익성 개선으로 
올해 북미 HEV와 유럽 BEV 중심 신차 출시 및 물량 확대가 본격화되면
서, ’27년에는 이익 성장 기여도가 확대될 것으로 전망. 미국 조 지 아 텔 루 라 이
드 HEV 신규 생산(2.8만대) 및 HMGMA 스 포 티 지HEV신규 생산(1.0만
대) 등 수 요 가 높은 북미 현 지 에 서 HEV 물량 확대 중으로 4Q에도 텔루라이
드 HEV와 스포티지 HEV의 현지 생산/판매 확대를 비롯하여 셀토스 HEV 
출시로 ’27년 북미 중심 HEV 신차 물량 증가 및 믹스 개선 기대. 유럽은 강한 
BEV 수요가 지속되면서 중국 업체들의 공세 강화 중이나 동사의 물량 확대 또
한 견조. 3Q EV3 2.9만대, EV5 2.2만대 등 유럽 BEV 수요 대응 전략 주효. ’27
년에는 K4/셀토스 및 쏘렌토 등 HEV 신차 중 심 의 친환경차 PT 믹스 다양화
로 유럽 내 물량 확대 흐름 지속 전망 
물량 확대는 증익으로 귀결, 기말 배당 매력도 부각 
’27년 동사 영업이익 추 정 치 는 11.1조원(+13.5%)으로 증익 지속 전망. 최근 
환율 추세 고려 시 내년 수익성에 부 정 적 영향 확대 가능성 존재하나, 올해 확
인된 신차 중심 물량 확대를 통해 부정적 환율 영향을 상쇄할 것 으 로 기대. 
12M EPS 추정치 하향(-2.9%) 및 상 반 기 적 용 한 코스피 프리미엄 제거(-
10%)로 목 표 주 가 를 260,000원으로 하향. 그러나, RA 설립에서의 동사 지분 
참여 및 기말 배당(’26년 DPS 7,000원 추정)에 따른 상 대 적 배당 수익 매력
도 부각 등은 주가 모 멘 텀 에 있어 기대 가능한 요소.

[ Compliance Notice ] (공표일: 2026 년 10월 8 일) 
이 자료는 조사분석 담당자가 객관적 사실에 근거해 작성하였으며, 타인의 부당한 압력이나 간섭없이 본인의 의견을 정확하게 
반영했습니다. 본인은 이 자료에서 다룬 종목과 관련해 공표일 현재 관련 법규상 알려야 할 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다. 본인은 이 
자료를 기관투자자 또는 제 3 자에게 사전에 제공한 사실이 없습니다. (김성래) 
저희 회사는 공표일 현재 이 자료에서 다룬 종목의 발행주식을 1% 이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다. 
이 자료는 투자자의 증권투자를 돕기 위해 당사 고객에 한하여 배포되는 자료로서 저작권이 당사에 있으며 불법 복제 및 배포를 금
합니다. 이 자료에 수록된 내용은 당사 리서치센터가 신뢰할 만한 자료나 정보출처로부터 얻은 것이지만, 당사는 그 정확성이나 완
전성을 보장할 수 없습니다. 따라서 이 자료는 어떠한 경우에도 고객의 증권투자 결과와 관련된 법적 책임소재에 대한 증빙으로 사
용될 수 없습니다.

기아 (000270) [한화리서치]

8 
[ 종목 투자등급 ] 
당사는 개별 종목에 대해 향후 1 년간 +15% 이상의 절대수익률이 기대되는 종목에 대해 Buy(매수) 의견을 제시합니다. 또한 절대수
익률 -15~+15%가 예상되는 종목에 대해 Hold(보유) 의견을, -15% 이하가 예상되는 종목에 대해 Sell(매도) 의견을 제시합니다. 밸류
에이션 방법 등 절대수익률 산정은 개별 종목을 커버하는 애널리스트의 추정에 따르며, 목표주가 산정이나 투자의견 변경 주기는 종
목별로 다릅니다.

[ 산업 투자의견 ] 
당사는 산업에 대해 향후 1 년간 해당 업종의 수익률이 과거 수익률에 비해 양호한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Positive(긍정
적) 의견을 제시하고 있습니다. 또한 향후 1 년간 수익률이 과거 수익률과 유사한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Neutral(중립적) 
의견을, 과거 수익률보다 부진한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Negative(부정적) 의견을 제시하고 있습니다. 산업별 수익률 전
망은 해당 산업 내 분석대상 종목들에 대한 담당 애널리스트의 분석과 판단에 따릅니다.

2026.10.30   
현대차 (005380)

26.3Q Review: 이제 올라가야 할 때 Company Brief

본업 Review: 26.3Q 매출액 45.1조원, 영업이익 2.6조원  
현대차는 26.3Q 매출액 45.1원 (-3.4% YoY, -8.3% QoQ), 영업이익 2.6조원 (+5.5% 
YoY, -6.1% QoQ , +5.9% OPM ) 등의 실적을 시현했다 [iM증권 추정치 매출액: 
45.3조원, 영업이익: 2.4조원]. 매출액은 시장 컨센서스에 부합했고, 영업이익의 경우 
시장 컨센서스였던 2.4조원을 소폭 상회했다. 주요 구성 요소 별 내용은 아래와 같다.  
① P: 26.3Q HEV 판매 대수는 16.6만대를 기록하며 전사 판매 대비 비중 
18.2% 달성 (+18.8% YoY, -11.1% QoQ). 미국의 경우 국내 공장 생산 
감소에 따른 수출 부진으로 도매 판매는 소폭 감소했으나, 판매 비중은 
25.2%로 전반적인 믹스는 직전 분기와 유사. 26.3Q 평균환율이 직전 분기 
대비 크게 하락했으나, 전년 동기 기저 탓에 오히려 + 환율 효과 관찰됨. 
② Q: 26.3Q 도매 판매 대수는 91.8만대로 전년 동기 대비 11.5% 감소 (이하 
YoY 기준, 미국: -10.0%, 유럽: -18.1% 국내: -27.2% 인도: + 18.3%). 
26.2Q 안전 공업 화재에 이어 26.3Q에 발생한 파업의 영향으로 생산 
대수는 감소. 국내 공장에서의 생산 차질은 내수 판매에 더해 수출 감소, 즉 
전체적인 판매 축소로 연결됨. 26.4Q부터 생산 관련 노이즈는 발생하지 
않을 전망. 이에 더해 아반떼, 투싼 등의 신차 판매 효과를 통한 반등 가능.  
③ C: 26.3Q 관세 비용은 직전 분기와 유사한 수준으로 발생한 것으로 파악 
(25.2Q 0.8조원, 25.3Q 1.8조원, 25.4Q 1.5조원, 26.1Q 0.8조원, 26.2Q 
0.9조원). 이란 사태에 따른 주요 원자재 가격 상승 압력은 계속 존재하나, 
내부 원가 절감 정책으로 약 5,000억원의 원가 상승분을 상쇄. 현대차는 
26.4Q 원가 절감 정책의 효과가 더 크게 나타날 것이라고 언급. 26.3Q 
기말 환율이 전년 동기 대비 급락함에 따라 판매보층충당부채전입액 또한 
감소 (26.3Q 매출원가, 판매관리비 각 전년 동기 대비 1.7%, 19.2% 감소). 
금융 부문의 경우 26.3Q 매출액 9.1조원 (+27.8% YoY) , 영업이익 4,710억원 (-
18.2% YoY, -37.9%) 등의 실적을 기록했다. 완성차 판매가 전반적으로 
위축되었음에도 외형 성장을 이어갔으나, 금리 상승에 따른 이자 비용 등 전반적인 
조달 관련 비용이 증가했다. 일부 EV를 중심으로 중고차 가격이 소폭 하락한 점 또한 
HCA 등 주요 부문 이익 감소의 배경이다. 26.3Q 현대차 순이익은 1,932억원을 
기록했는데, 이는 영업외손익이 전년 동기 789억원에서 229억원으로 감소했기 
때문이다. 현대차는 주요 환율 하락에 따른 외화환산손실 증가의 영향이라고 밝혔다. 
1) 생산 단에서의 노이즈가 연속해 발생하며 내수 판매 및 수출 감소가 불가피했으나, 
2) 원자재 가격 상승 영향을 내부 원가 절감 정책을 통해 상쇄했고, 3) 주요 
평균환율이 하락했으나 기말환율 또한 급락하며 판매보증충당부채전입액 감소 효과가 
나타나 시장 기대를 소폭 상회하는 수익성이 관찰되었다는 것이 26.3Q 실적 총평이다. 
현대차는 2026년 도매 판매 가이던스를 416만대에서 400만대로 하향 조정했다. 
26.3Q 누적 도매 판매 대수는 288만대이기에 하향 조정은 불가피했다. 단 신규 
가이던스에 따르면 26.4Q 판매 목표는 약 110만대 수준이라는 것인데, 아반떼, 투싼, 
주요 모델 페이스리프트, GV80 HEV 등의 신차 효과가 얼마나 나타날지가 관건일 
것이다 (이하 분기 도매 판매, 25.1Q 100.1만대, 25.2Q 105.7만대, 25.3Q 103.8만대).  
Buy (Maintain)

현대차 (005380)
2
미래 사업 업데이트: 자율주행은 계획대로, Atlas는 조금씩 변신중
26.3Q 컨퍼런스 콜에서 특별히 SDV~Boston Dynamics 관련 업데이트는 확인되지
않았으나 여러 매체를 통해 파악된 현대차그룹의 미래 사업 진행 상황은 다음과 같다.
① SDV/자율주행: 데이터 플라이 휠에 대한 중요성 강조. 27년 말부터 광주
권역 자율주행 실증 사업에 투입되는 차량은 Atria AI 활용. SDV 플랫폼이
적용된 차량 양산 시작은 29년. 양산 SDV 출하량 증가함에 따라 29년부터
GPU 5 만개 이상으로 구성되는 새만금 데이터센터를 자체 운영을 목표함.
박민우 포티투닷 사장 겸 AVP 본부장이 부임한 이후 Tesla, nVIDIA 출신의
개발 인력을 충원 중인 상황. 26.05 그랜저 페이스리프트 모델에 플레오스
탑재 시작. 26.09부터 판매되고 있는 신형 아반떼에도 플레오스 탑재 완료.

② Boston Dynamics: 10.8 Rohit Prasad를 CEO로 선임. Rohit Prasad은
아마존에서 Alex 개발에 중추적인 역할을 수행했던 인물. 현대차그룹에서
직접 하드웨어 개발 및 양산을 챙기고, 소프트웨어에 역량을 높이겠다는
행보로 판단. 26.10 Atlas의 신규 덱스트러스를 공개했는데, 기존 제품 대비
자유도가 크게 증가 (7DoF→14DoF). 순수 직구동 방식을 여전히
고수함.10.27 Boston Dynamics가 주요 부품 업체에게 Atlas 1만대 물량의
부품 PO를 곧 발송할 것이라는 기사가 보도됨. 28년까지 연 3만대 CAPA의
공장 완공이라는 기존 계획 대비 양산 및 개발 속도가 빨라진다는 시그널임.
현대차그룹의 미래 사업 업데이트를 또 찾아볼 수 있을 가장 빠른 이벤트는 CES
2027이다. 다만 CES 2027에는 현대차가 아닌 그룹사가 참여함에 따라 해당 행사에서

유의미한 업데이트 내용은 찾아보기 힘들 것이며 이에 중장기 관점의 접근이 필요하다.
시장의 우려가 반영된 주가 하락: 빨라질 속도와 올라올 본업에 주목
현대차 주가는 26.2H 접어들며 크게 하락하고 있고, CES 2026 전후의 상승폭을 사실상
모두 반납했다 (연초 기준 -1.3%, 26.2H 이후 -41.7%). 또한 최근 시장 반등
흐름에서도 사실상 소외되고 있다. 현대차를 바라보는 투자자들의 우려는 아래와 같다.
첫째, CID 전후로 Boston Dynamics 관련 업데이트가 너무 부족하다는 것이다. 26.08
현대차 CID 행사를 앞두고 많은 투자자들은 빅테크 업체들의 지분 투자 및 Atlas
개발과 관련해 새로운 내용을 기대한 것이 사실이다. 이런 상황에서 유럽, 내수 등 주요
지역 에서의 판매 업황 또한 좋지 않다 보니, 시장의 실망은 더욱 컸다. 전술한 것과
같이 CES 2027에 현대차가 참여하지 않는다는 점에서 내년에 대한 기대도 크지 않다.

둘째, 내수와 유럽 업황이 그룹사 대비 너무 좋지 못하다는 점이다. 26.3Q 현대차 내수,
유럽 도매 판매는 전년 동기 대비 각 27.2%, 18.1% 감소했다. 반면 26.3Q 기아 내수,
유럽 도매 판매는 전년 동기 대비 각 2.2%, 18.7% 증가했다. 특히 유럽에서의 부진은
EV 라인업이 상대적으로 빈약하고, 중국 업체와의 경쟁이 심화되고 있다는 구조적
문제에서 출발하기에, 투자자들은 26년을 넘어 27, 28년의 성장까지 우려하고 있다
단 우리는 Boston D ynamics의 Atlas는 양산이라는 방향성이 설정된지 오래되지
않았다는 점을 기억해야 한다. 초기 단계부터 양산을 위해 개발된 Tesla Optimus 대비
개발 속도가 빠를 수는 없다. 1~2만대 수준의 데모 제품이 확보되고, 이를 바탕으로
데이터 수집이 일정 부분 확보되는 이후부터 관련 사업 확대 속도는 빨라 질것이다.

취약 지역 판매 업황의 경우에도 내수를 시작으로 점진적으로 개선될 것이다. 생산 차질
외에도 Tesla 및 중국 OEM들의 시장 침투가 우려됨은 분명하지만, 주력 차종 중에서도
국내 판매 볼륨이 큰 아반떼와 투싼의 신차 효과는 분명 기대되는 지점이다. 유럽에서의
반등은 시간이 필요할 것이나, 일단 아이오닉 3가 26.4Q부터 본격적으로 판매될
예정이다. 단 이는 큰 변동 없이 IAA가 2028년부터 시행된다는 전제에 근거하고 있다.
현대차 목표주가 600,000원 유지: 여전한 Multiple 할증 요인
현대차 목표주가를 600,000원으로 유지한다. 26년 EPS 기준 6~8배 수준의 P/E
Multiple을 부여받고 있는 Peer Group들은 중국 시장 M/S 축소로 수익성이 급락하고
있고, 미래 사업 경쟁력은 여전히 전무하다. 현대차의 Multiple 할증 요인은 충분하다.

그림17. 유럽 생산 OEM 생산원가 추이 
 `  
자료: iM증권 리서치본부

IAA는 EU판 IRA로 볼 수 있음. 현대차그룹의 경우 현대차 (터키, 체코)와 기아 (슬로바키아) 모두 유렵 현지
생산 라인을 이미 확보했고, 현대모비스를 중심으로 한 부품 자급률 비중 이미 높음. 또한 주요 부품에 있어
FTA 체결국의 원산지를 EU산과 동일하게 적용하는 규정 존재. 실제 발효까지 큰 변수가 없다면 국내 완성차
업체의 경우 구조적으로 수혜 클 것. 단 중국 업체들에 대한 명시적 배제 규정이 없다는 점은 한계 중 하나. 
IAA 주요 조항 주요 내용
적용 대상
공공조달 차량, 공적 지원(구매 보조금 등)을 받는 차량, 법인차가 대상임. 전기
차, PHEV, 수소차에 적용됨. 2028년부터 회원국은 무공해 및 저공해이면서 EU
산인 차량이 아니면 법인차를 지원할 수 없음. 판매 금지가 아니라 수요 측 혜택
을 역내 생산과 연동하는 구조
역내 부품 요건
1단계(발효 6개월 후)는 EU 조립, 배터리 제외 부품가치 70% EU산, 셀 포함
배터리 핵심부품 3개 EU산을 요구함. 2단계(1단계 3년 후)는 배터리 핵심부품
5개(셀, 양극재,BMS 포함)와 전동 파워트레인·전장 가치 50% EU산이 추가됨.
소형차 특례
전장 4.2m 이하 전기 승용차가 대상임. EU 조립에 더해 부품가치 70%와
배터리 핵심부품 3개 중 하나만 충족하면 됨. 충족 시 CO2 규제상 1.3대로 인정
됨(2025~34년).
FDI 조건 1억 유로 초과 투자가 대상이며, 지분 49% 상한, EU 인력 50% 이상, EU산 투입재 30% 이상 등의 조건이 붙음.
FT A·관세동맹국 예외
FT A나 관세동맹을 맺은 국가의 부품은 EU산과 동등한 부품으로 인정되며,
정부조달협정(GPA) 가입국도 일부 조항에 포함됨. 단 최종 조립국까지 인정되
는지는 불명확하며, 조문상으로는 EU, EEA 역내 조립만 인정되는 것으로 해석
됨. 따라서 한국산 부품은 EU산으로 인정 가능하나, 한국이나 튀르키예에서 조
립한 완성차는 요건 미충족 가능성이 높음.
FT A 예외 배제 조건
집행위는 위임법으로 특정국을 동등 대상에서 제외할 수 있음. 해당국이 EU 기업
에 내국민 대우를 하지 않는 경우, 공급망 의존도나 공급 안보 위협이 있는 경우,
해당 협정의 예외 조항에 해당하는 경우가 배제 사유임. 중국은 FT A가 없어 애초
에 대상이 아님.
2020 2021 2022 2023
대당 생산원가 30,683 32,029 35,079 38,140
대당 판매가격 34,404 27,557 31,244 33,560
1인당 인건비 70,981 74,904 78,348 88,198
통상적으로 중국 OEM의 생산 원가 (중국 생산 가정)은 글로벌 OEM 대비 30% 낮다고 추정됨. BYD의 경우 돌핀 등과 같
은 C 세그먼트 차량에서의 유럽 수익성이 낮지만, A~B 세그먼트 급의 돌핀 서프의 경우 매우 높은 수익성 시현. 이는 유럽
시장 내의 A~B 세그먼트 경쟁 강도가 낮다는 점에서 기인. 즉 100% 수준의 관세가 부과되지 않는다면 BYD의 가격 경쟁
력은 유지되는 것. 따라서 EU는 2,000유로 수준의 중국 OEM의 대당 이익을 상쇄하기 위해 이에 준하는 수준으로 지급되
는 EV 구매 보조금 및 법인 차량 세제혜택을 검토 중인 것이고, 이를 IAA에 담아낼지가 핵심.

그림23. 26.3Q 국내 완성차 실적 Summary

자료: iM증권 리서치본부

현대차 미주: 펠리세이드 리콜 이슈 존재하나, 메타플랜트 및 기존 앨러배마 공
장 HEV 생산을 통한 시장 진출 속도 빨라질 것으로 전망
인도: GM 탈레가온 공장 인수를 통한 생산량 확대에 나설 예정이고, 이
를 통한 인도 내수 수요 대응 및 글로벌 신흥 시장 진출 또한 목표
유럽: 기아 대비 EV 라인업의 폭에 열위에 있으나, 캐스퍼 EV, EV 3를
통해 EV 수요 회복 국면을 앞두고 있는 유럽 시장 대응에 나설 예정
기아 미주: 텔룰라이드 HEV 출시를 통해 Mid-Size  HEV 수요 대응 이뤄지
고 있음. 스포티지 및 셀토스 등 HEV를 통한 기존 차급 대응 강화 전망
인도: 2030년까지 10개 차종 출시 목표하고 있고, 이를 통해 2030년
41만대 도매 판매, 시장 점유율 7.6% 달성 가이던스를 제시함
유럽: EV2 출시를 통한 EV 라인업 완성도 높임. 소형 해치백 차량과 신
규 HEV 차량 출시, 그리고 PBV 판매 본격화를 통한 초과 성장 목표

 금융투자분석사와 그 배우자는 해당 기업의 주식을 보유하고 있지 않습니다.  
 당 보고서는 기관투자가 및 제 3자에게 E-mail등을 통하여 사전에 배포된 사실이 없습니다.  
 회사는 6개월간 해당 기업의 유가증권 발행과 관련 주관사로 참여하지 않았습니다. 
 당 보고서에 게재된 내용들은 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭 없이 작성되었음을 확인합니다. 
본 분석자료는 투자자의 증권투자를 돕기 위한 참고자료이며, 따라서, 본 자료에 의한 투자자의 투자결과에 대해 어떠한 목적의 증빙자료로도 사용될 수 없으며, 어떠한 경우에도 작성자 및 
당사의 허가 없이 전재, 복사 또는 대여될 수 없습니다. 무단전재 등으로 인한 분쟁발생시 법적 책임이 있음을 주지하시기 바랍니다.

[투자의견] 
종목추천 투자등급  
종목투자의견은 향후 12개월간 추천일 종가대비 해당종목의 예상 목표수익률을 의미함.  
 Buy(매수): 추천일 종가대비 +15% 이상  
 Hold(보유): 추천일 종가대비 -15% ~ 15% 내외 등락 
 Sell(매도): 추천일 종가대비 -15% 이상 
산업추천 투자등급  
시가총액기준 산업별 시장비중대비 보유비중의 변화를 추천하는 것임 
 Overweight(비중확대) 
 Neutral(중립) 
 Underweight(비중축소)

[투자등급 비율 2026-09-30 기준]

## Document 263: [329180] HD현대중공업(329180) 피크아웃 우려를 이겨낼 조선사HD현대중공업(329180) 피크아웃 우려를 이겨낼 조선사피크아웃 우려를 이겨낼 조선사 - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[329180] HD현대중공업(329180) 피크아웃 우려를 이겨낼 조선사HD현대중공업(329180) 피크아웃 우려를 이겨낼 조선사피크아웃 우려를 이겨낼 조선사 - 기업.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-10-29
Pages: 4
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

(329180) 
HD 현대중공업 
2026.10.29

AI로보틱스/방산/조선/운송 양승윤 
6139 / syyang0901@eugenefn.com

피크아웃 우려를 이겨낼 조선사 
 투자의견 BUY(유지) 
 목표주가 610,000원(하향) 
 현재주가 285,500원(10/28)

 3Q26 매출액은 5조 7,536억원(+30.2%yoy), 영업이익은 8,992억원
(+61.3%yoy, OPM 15.6%)을 기록. 영업이익 컨센서스 9,792억원 하회.  
 평균 환율이 약 79원 하락하며 영업이익 약 450억원이 줄었고, 조업
일수는 47일로 통상 계절성 대비 4일 추가 감소해 약 500억원의 이
익 감소 효과 발생. 임단협 추가 비용의 약 75%도 3분기에 선반영
(잔여분은 4분기 반영). 일회성 요인을 제외하면  이익 체력은 견조. 
 동사의 조선 부문 매출액은 4조 3,988억원(+44.0%yoy), 영업이익은 
6,463억원(+62.8%yoy, OPM 14.7%)을 기록. 상선 OPM은 14.7%로 
2Q26(17.8%) 대비 하락했으나 대부분 외생변수 영향. 3Q26 대형선 매출 
중 2023년 수주분 비중은 45%, 2024~2025년 수주분은 51%. 고선가 
물량의 매출 비중이 높아지는 2027년까지 수익성 개선 여력은 충분.  
 9월 말 누적 상선 수주는 138.0억달러로 연간 목표의 120%, 엔진기계는 
41.4억달러로 155%를 달성했으나 함정(13%)과 해양(5%)은 부진. LNG선
은 중국 조선소의 저가 수주와 발주 둔화로 선가가 일부 하락. 선주들이 현 
선가를 저점으로 인식하며 발주 협의를 재개하는 분위기. 북미 LNG 프로젝
트 FID가 이어지고 있어 2027년 LNG선 발주 회복 기대. 
 함정 부문은 매출 2,570억원(-14.6%qoq)으로 줄었으나, 수출 비중 확대에 
따른 믹스 개선으로 영업이익 386억원(+114%qoq, OPM 15.0%)을 기록. 
해양플랜트는 매출액 3,799억원(+35.3%yoy), 영업이익 1,265억원(OPM 
33.3%). C/O 180억원을 제외해도 Ruya 공정 본격화와 Trion 고수익 물량
으로 높은 수익성 유지. 2027년 일부 프로젝트 인도로 매출 감소가 예상되
나, LNG선·블록 물량 투입으로 고정비 부담을 완화할 계획. 신규 해상변전
소(OSS) 계약을 앞두고 있으며, 2027년 초 추가 수주 발표도 기대. 
 주목할 부분은 엔진기계. 영업이익 2,299억원(OPM 24.2%)으로 높은 D/F 
엔진 비중을 바탕으로 견조한 수익성 유지. 육상발전용 힘센엔진은 올해   
3GW를 확보하며 2029년까지 계획 캐파 수준의 물량을 이미 채움. 생산 
예상 물량은 2028년 약 1.5GW, 2029년 약 3GW이며, 신규 공장 구축 이
후 연간 생산능력은 약 4GW로 확대 예정. 북미 Hyperscaler와의 직접 협
상이 늘고 있으며, 힘센엔진을 활용한 부유식 발전설비(BMPP)로 FDC 시장
까지 대응 가능. AI 데이터센터 전력 수요가 2028년 이후 새로운 이익 축. 
 미국에서는 Kiewit과 합작법인 설립 계약 체결. 군수지원함 등 지원함 사업
부터 추진하며, 현지 조선소 건설 과정에서 HII에 선박 블록을 공급하는 방
안도 협의 중. 구체적인 투자계획은 2026년 말까지 확정 목표. 국내에서는 
핵추진잠수함 초기 예산이 2027년 반영되며 동사도 사업 참여를 준비 중. 
 동사에 대한 투자의견 BUY, 목표주가 61만원을 제시. 고선가 물량 매출 확
대와 육상발전용 엔진 성장으로 피크아웃을 이겨낼 굴지의 조선사.

## Document 278: AI・半導体で韓国景気は堅調も、「Ｋ字型」の様相が一段と鮮明に ～家計部門の低迷が李政権の重しとなる可能性～

Source PDF: all report pdf/AI・半導体で韓国景気は堅調も、「Ｋ字型」の様相が一段と鮮明に ～家計部門の低迷が李政権の重しとなる可能性～.pdf
Report source: Unknown
Date: 未知日期
Pages: 5
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Asia Trends

2026 年10 月 27 日

1/5 
AI・半導体で韓国景気は堅調も、「Ｋ字型」の様相が一段と鮮明に 
～家計部門の低迷が李政権の重しとなる可能性～ 
 経済調査部 主席エコノミスト  西濵 徹 
（℡：050-5474-7495）

（要旨） 
○ 韓国景気は、世界的なＡＩ・半導体関連需要を追い風に、外需主導で堅調な推移が続いてい
る。 ７-９月の実質ＧＤＰ成長率は前期比＋1.0％ （年率換算＋3.93％） 、 前年同期比＋3.4％
となり、2026 年９月までの累計成長率（＋3.6％）は中銀の８月時点の見通し（＋3.3％）を
上回った。メモリ半導体を中心とする財輸出に加え、半導体製造設備やデータセンター関連
の設備投資が成長を支え、2027 年も外需の堅調さが景気をけん引する展開が見込まれる。 
○ 一方、家計部門は力強さを欠いている。インフレ率は中銀目標の２％を上回って推移し、政
府の現金給付などの消費支援も効果が一巡したことから、個人消費は下振れた。成果給の恩
恵は一部の労働者にとどまり、業種間の賃金格差の拡大も懸念される。ソウルを中心に不動
産価格の上昇が続くなか、 家計債務を巡る金融リスクもくすぶっており、 中銀は10 月会合で
政策金利を据え置きつつも、追加利上げに含みを持たせた。KOSPI はピークから約４割下落
しており、金融市場の調整が家計に与える影響にも注意が必要である。 
○ 為替市場では、株価調整とは対照的に、ウォン相場が底堅く推移している。過去最高を更新
した貿易黒字に伴う実需と、中銀のタカ派姿勢が下支えとなっている。 
○ 政治面では、李在明政権の支持率が過去最低に近い水準にある。企業と家計、資産保有層と
それ以外の層との間で景気回復の恩恵に格差が生じる「Ｋ字型」の様相が強まっており、金
融市場の混乱が家計部門に波及すれば、政権の求心力が一段と低下する可能性がある。

【世界的なAI・半導体需要の旺盛さを追い風に、足元の韓国景気は堅調な推移が続いている】 
韓国では、 世界的なＡＩ （人工知能） ・ 半導体関連需要の旺盛な動きが景気の追い風となる展開が
続いている（注１）。韓国は、DRAM やNAND 型フラッシュメモリ、広帯域メモリ（ＨＢＭ）などメモ
リ半導体の大規模量産で世界トップクラスの競争力を有する。旺盛な半導体需要をけん引役に９月
の輸出額は過去最高額を更新するなど、輸出の勢いは強まっている。韓国はＧＤＰに占める輸出の

注１ 10 月１日付レポート「韓国､AI・半導体需要が景気に追い風も､政治への逆風は止まず」 
  （https://www.dlri.co.jp/report/macro/666170.html）

### Page 2

2/5 
比率が４割を上回るなど、 アジア太平洋
地域のなかでも外需依存度が相対的に
高い。こうしたなか、 ７-９月の実質ＧＤ
Ｐ成長率は前期比＋1.0％、年率換算ベ
ースでは＋3.93％となり、 景気の堅調さ
が確認されている （図１）。 中期的な基調
を示す前年同期比ベースの成長率も＋
3.4％と前期（同＋3.7％）から伸びが鈍
化しているものの、 ３四半期連続で３％
を上回る水準で推移している。 
世界的なＡＩ・半導体関連需要の拡大
を反映して財輸出が押し上げられるとともに（図２）、中国人観光客を中心とする来訪者数の増加
はサービス輸出を押し上げるなど、堅調な外需が景気をけん引する動きがみられる。さらに、 ＡＩ ・
半導体関連需要の旺盛さを追い風に、 半導体製造設備やデータセンター関連の投資が拡大するなど、
設備投資も景気を下支えしている。ま
た、政府消費の増加も成長に寄与した。
一方で、政府はイラン情勢の悪化を受け
た原油高の影響軽減を目的に、低所得者
層などを対象に１人当たり 10 万～60 万
ウォンの現金給付を実施したほか、 省エ
ネ家電の購入促進策 （キャッシュバック
の実施）などの消費支援に動いた。しか
し、足元ではその効果が一巡しており、
個人消費は下振れしている。さらに、前
期は在庫調整が進む動きが確認された
ものの、 当期は前期比年率ベースで＋0.32pt と試算されるなど、在庫の積み上がりを示唆する動き
もうかがえる。 
とはいえ、2026 年の９月までの累計ベースの経済成長率は＋3.6％となり、中銀が８月時点で示
した見通し （＋3.3％）を上回っている。 中銀は10 月会合において、2026 年の経済成長率が８月時
点の見通しを大きく上回る可能性を示唆したが、 足元の状況はそうした見方に沿った動きと捉えら
れる。 さらに、2027 年についても、 世界的なＡＩ・半導体関連需要の動向に留意する必要があるも
のの、外需の堅調さが景気をけん引する展開が続く可能性が見込まれる。

### Page 3

3/5 
【家計部門はインフレに直面、賃金上昇の恩恵は一部にとどまり、不動産高騰や株価調整も重し】 
外需は堅調な動きをみせる一方、対照的に個人消費は力強さを欠いている。背景には、2025 年後
半以降のインフレ率が中銀目標（２％）を上回る伸びで推移するなど、実質購買力に下押し圧力が
かかっていることがある。さらに、イラ
ン情勢の悪化を受けた 原油高を背景に
物価上昇圧力が残り、インフレ率は中銀
目標を上回る水準で推移している （図
３） 。これを受けて、政府は燃料価格の上
限規制 を約６ヵ月維持するなど物価対
策に動いている。しかし、足元では中東
情勢 の不透明 感を理由に 原油価格は高
止まりするなど、見通しが立たない状況
が続いている。一方で、半導体産業など
一部企業では、営業利益などの一定割合
（Ｎ％）を従業員の成果報酬の原資とする「Ｎ％成果給」が導入され、他産業にも導入を求める動
きが広がっている。 とはいえ、実際に高額な成果報酬の恩恵を受ける労働者は一部にとどまるなど、
業種間の賃金格差の拡大に繋がる懸念も高まっている。 
韓国ではここ数年、首都ソウルを中心とする不動産価格の高騰が社会問題化してきた。 政府は
2025 年以降に 様々な不動産高騰対策を
打ち出したものの、一連の対策では実需
層の資金調達が困難になる一方、規制対
象外の地域で不動産価格が上昇するな
ど「副作用」も顕在化した。また、政府
は超高額住宅や所有者が居住していな
い住宅に対する課税強化を目指したも
のの、中高所得者層などからの反発を受
けて一部見直しを余儀なくされた。さら
に、一連の政府主導による不動産対策に
対する不満は、６月に実施されたソウル
市長選で与党候補が敗北した一因との見方もある。とはいえ、 足元においてもソウルを中心とする
不動産価格は上昇が続いており （図４） 、住宅関連融資の増加が続くなど、 家計債務を巡る金融リス
クもくすぶっている。中銀は、10 月の定例会合で３会合ぶりに政策金利を据え置いたものの、 追加
利上げに含みを持たせる考えを示した（注２）。背景のひとつには、 不動産市場を巡るリスクがある

注２ 10 月22 日付レポート「韓国中銀、株価急落などで利上げ休止も、金融引き締めの長期化を示唆」 
  （https://www.dlri.co.jp/report/macro/666810.html）

### Page 4

4/5 
と考えられる。 
2025 年以降の韓国金融市場では、世界的なＡＩ・半導体関連需要の旺盛な動きを追い風に、主要
株価指数（KOSPI）は上昇基調を強めた。
さらに、 政府は2025 年末、 個人投資家が
保有する海外株式を売却して国内株式
に１年間投資した場合、一定の条件の下
で譲渡所得税を最大 100％控除する時限
措置を発表し、2026 年３月に法制化し
た。そのうえ、株価上昇を受けて個人投
資家の間でレバレッジ投資が活発化し
たことも、株価上昇の動きを後押しする
一助となった。しかし、構成銘柄全体の
時価総額に占める割合が５割前後を占
めるサムスン電子とＳＫハイニックスの２社の株価動向にKOSPI が左右されやすい構造にある。 こ
うしたなか、足元ではピークから約４割下落するなど、市場は大幅な調整に直面している（図５）
（注３）。こうした金融市場の不安定な動きも、家計部門に悪影響を与えている可能性がある。

【株価の大幅調整が続いているものの、貿易黒字と中銀のタカ派姿勢がウォン相場を下支え】 
金融市場においては、前述の通り半導体関連株を中心とする株価調整の動きに直面しているもの
の、 通貨ウォン相場は対照的に底堅い動きをみせている （図６） 。 背景には、世界的なＡＩ・半導体
関連需要の旺盛さが輸出を押し上げる
なか、足元の貿易黒字は過去最高を更新
するなど、ウォンに対する実需が生まれ
やすい環境にあることが挙げられる。ま
た、中銀が断続的な利上げを実施すると
ともに、先行きも追加利上げに含みを持
たせるなど「タカ派」姿勢を強めている
ことがある。さらに、 中銀の申総裁は10
月の定例会合後に行った記者会見にお
いて、「足元のウォンの対ドル相場は歴
史的に見れば低水準」 、 「実質実効ベース
でもウォンは依然割安」、「ウォン高は経済的不平等を是正する効果がある」と述べるなど、ウォン
高の経済的な利点を指摘したことも、ウォン相場を後押ししている可能性がある。

注３ 10 月15 日付レポート「韓国株が急落、AI・半導体好況の陰で市場の調整が深まる」 
  （https://www.dlri.co.jp/report/macro/666600.html）

5/5
【金融市場の混乱や家計部門を取り巻く環境が李在明政権の行方を左右する可能性に要注意】
こうしたなか、李在明（イ・ジェミョン）政権の支持率は低水準で推移しており、足元では過去
最低に近い水準で推移している。 このところの支持率低下を巡っては、 若年層などで支持が弱まる
動きがみられるほか、 不動産政策や物価高への不満も影響していると考えられる。 こうした動きは、
政権が支持層をつなぎとめることの難しさを示唆している。足元の景気はいわゆる「Ｋ字型」の様
相を強めるとともに、 企業と家計、 資産保有層とそれ以外の層との間で景気回復の恩恵に格差が生
じており、その格差が一段と拡大する兆しもみられる。このため、先行きは実体経済の堅調さにも

かかわらず、李政権を取り巻く環境が厳しさを増す可能性も考えられる。与党・共に民主党を巡っ
ても、党内では政策運営などを巡る意見対立がみられるなど、火種を抱えている。金融市場の混乱
が家計部門に波及することで、 李政権の求心力をさらに弱める可能性がある点には注意が必要であ
る。

MACRO STRATEGY
The Yen, 10-Year Yield and Iran:  Bessent’s Three-Front War and the Stakes for Investors
Joseph Quinlan, Managing Director and Head of Market Strategy
Scott Bessent may carry the title of Treasury Secretary, but these days he looks more like
America’s financial field marshal. His battlefield stretches from Tokyo, where he is trying
to engineer a stronger Japan yen; to the U.S. Treasury market, where he is battling to keep

the 10-year yield from breaking decisively above 5%; and Tehran, where he has become the
point man in the escalating U.S. campaign to squeeze Iran financially and economically.
Make no mistake about it: Secretary Bessent and the Treasury Department are increasingly
an instrument of American economic statecraft, charged not simply with financing the
government and overseeing the financial system, but with managing markets, currencies
and geopolitical adversaries simultaneously. Welcome to the new age of financial warfare.

Front One: Defending the Yen. The first front unfolded over the summer when Secretary
Bessent and the BoJ stealthily intervened in support of the yen, which had fallen to a near
40-year low against the dollar by late July. To arrest the yen’s decline, the U.S. Treasury sold
euros instead of dollars in support of the yen, while pushing the BoJ to make use of the
Fed’s emergency repo facilities. In further support of the yen, the BoJ did what Bessent and

the markets have been pining for, raising its official rate by 25 basis points (bps) to 1.25%.
Since intervening in the foreign exchange markets, the yen has appreciated by nearly 5%
against the U.S. dollar, alleviating concerns of a disorderly unwind of the yen and giving
Bessent—thus far—an important victory. Defending the yen, of course, isn’t simply about
Japan—it’s about the U.S. and the need for foreign capital inflows.
Japan is one of America’s largest foreign creditors. A persistently weak yen creates

pressure on Tokyo to defend its currency, potentially by selling some of its enormous
holdings of foreign assets, including U.S. Treasurys. That prospect didn’t sit well with the
Treasury secretary. At precisely the moment Washington needs foreign capital to finance
its enormous fiscal deficits, the last thing Mr. Bessent wants is one of America’s largest
creditors to become a significant seller of Treasury securities. Hence, the battle to backstop

Japan’s currency.
Front Two: Defending the 10-Year Bond. Mr. Bessent has had less success on the
second front: the U.S. bond market. While the 10-year Treasury yield slipped slightly
following last week’s decision by the Fed to boost the fed funds rate by 25 bps, the
benchmark interest rate is up roughly 85 bps since the start of the year. Stubbornly high
inflation, elevated oil prices, enormous government borrowing requirements and growing
investor anxiety over America’s fiscal trajectory have all converged and conspired to keep

the 10-year yield closer to 5% than 4%.
Field Marshal Bessent is very aware that the 10-year Treasury is the gravitational center of
global finance—and that mortgage rates, corporate borrowing costs, equity valuations and
the government’s own interest bill all take their cue from it. A sustained move materially
above 5% would tighten financial conditions across the American economy, potentially
threaten the Equity bull market, and pile more pressure on the Republican Party.

To prevent this from happening, the Treasury has expanded its bond-buyback operations,
including purchases of longer-dated government securities, in an effort to improve liquidity
and relieve some pressure on the market, while shifting new government debt issuance
into short-term bills. Yet the bond vigilantes have so far proved harder to intimidate than
currency traders. Even after Treasury announced additional purchases, yields continued
climbing before receiving some relief from the latest hawkish move from the Fed.

Over the medium term, if investors believe Washington is borrowing too much, inflation is
running too hot or the supply of bonds is overwhelming global demand, they will demand a
higher yield to own America’s debt. This battle is far from over.
Front Three: Squeezing Tehran. Then there is Iran, where Mr. Bessent is wielding an
entirely different weapon: America’s control over the plumbing of global finance. In practice
Investment Implications
We expect continued investor

support and preference for real hard
assets, and a bias towards a plethora
of commodities and industrial plays.

### Page 3

3 of 8 September 21, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
this means the Treasury can make a foreign bank choose between doing business
with Iran or the U.S. It can freeze assets, blacklist companies, restrict transactions and
pressure allies to close financial escape routes.
Under “Operation Economic Outcast,” billed as economic “D-Day,” Treasury has
intensified sanctions against Iranian entities and financial networks, including recent

actions against Iran aviation and additional efforts aimed at banks and organizations
facilitating Tehran’s international transactions. Mr. Bessent has described the objective
in unusually stark terms: to “economically asphyxiate” the Iran regime. This is economic
warfare in its purest form, but whether it works remains to be seen.
“Asphyxiating” an economy takes time and patience—and international cooperation
and coordination. It means closing multiple global loopholes of third-party entities still

willing and able to do business with Iran—look no further than Russian banks. In addition,
squeezing a major oil producer runs the risk of aggravating global oil supplies, keeping
oil prices elevated. Higher energy costs, in turn, feed right back into the U.S. inflation
outlook the Fed is trying to tame and keeps the bond vigilantes on guard and ready to
push back. Not unlike the battle to suppress U.S. bond yields, the battle to bring Iran to
heel is going to be a long one.

How does it all end? How Scott Bessent’s three-front war plays out remains to be seen. A
decisive victory on all three fronts is unlikely since corralling currency and bond markets
is next to impossible, while the whims of geopolitics can lay waste to the best-laid plans.
That said, however, the U.S. Treasury Secretary has signaled that he is ready to take
a very proactive, pro-growth approach to his job. That’s the good news. The less
encouraging news stems from the inconsistencies in the overarching objectives of the

Treasury secretary.
Mr. Bessent can pressure Iran because access to the dollar-based financial system
remains extraordinarily valuable. He can intervene in foreign-exchange markets because
America’s financial resources give Washington enormous clout. And he can attempt to
influence the Treasury market because U.S. government securities remain the foundation
of the global financial system. But therein lies the contradiction.
The U.S. is simultaneously weaponizing the financial system abroad while becoming more

dependent on that same financial system at home. Washington wants the world to fear
losing access to dollars while also asking the world to lend it trillions of dollars. It wants to
use Treasury and dollar dominance as instruments of geopolitical coercion while running
fiscal deficits that require foreigners to continue believing deeply in those very assets.
The more Washington borrows, the more sensitive it becomes to bond-market discipline.
The more aggressively it weaponizes access to the dollar, the greater the incentive for

adversaries to seek alternatives. And the more the Treasury secretary intervenes directly
in markets, the more investors may begin wondering where economic policy ends and
market management begins.

For the moment, Mr. Bessent is fighting on three fronts. He wants a stronger yen, a
lower 10-year Treasury yield and an economically isolated Iran. Success on any one front
would be difficult. Achieving all three simultaneously will require considerable skill—and
perhaps a little luck.

MARKET VIEW
Beyond AI’s Resource Footprint, Consider its “Handprint”
Anna Potts, CFA, Vice President and Investment Strategist
Since the launch of ChatGPT, investors have celebrated AI’s potential while growing
more concerned about its resource footprint: Behind every AI-generated response is
a physical network of data centers, power plants, transmission lines, cooling systems,

semiconductor facilities and critical-mineral supply chains. No doubt, the AI revolution
has environmental costs. As adoption accelerates, growing demand for power, water,
advanced hardware and raw materials could strain grids, raise emissions and intensify
pressure on local resources. Yet the sustainability conversation should extend beyond
AI’s footprint to consider whether its environmental benefits can outweigh its
resource demands.
AI’s footprint is large, growing and certainly not trivial. AI increases electricity demand,

adds large, concentrated loads to already constrained grids, and may slow the retirement
of polluting energy assets. U.S. data centers could account for 20% of national electricity
consumption by 2035, up from 5.9% today, with power needs reaching 194 gigawatts
(comparable to the electricity demand of more than 150 million homes). 1 Cooling, power
generation and semiconductor manufacturing require water; each new megawatt also
embeds substantial copper, aluminum and other materials; and short hardware cycles

can increase electronic waste. Use varies sharply by workload: Complex reasoning and
agentic tasks can consume many times more energy than a simple text prompt. The
effects are already reaching consumers and communities. Wholesale power costs on
PJM—the largest U.S. grid—rose 76% year-over-year in Q1 2026, while data-center
load included in its previous two capacity auctions added an estimated $13.8 billion to
customer bills. 2 Communities are mobilizing around their concerns, including affordability,

water use and air pollution: Q1 2026 produced the largest single quarterly concentration
of delayed or blocked data-center projects on record. 3 Opposition is therefore becoming
a development constraint to be considered.
Context, caveats and comparisons. Headlines often tell only part of the story. Globally,
data centers account for about 1% of electricity use, 4 modest relative to many industries,
and 9% of estimated global 2025 to 2030 electricity growth. 5 A typical text prompt on

one major company’s AI model 6 uses 0.24 watt-hours of electricity, emits 0.03 grams
of CO₂ and consumes 0.26 milliliters of water—roughly 617,000 prompts match the
emissions from producing and cooking one sirloin steak, while 262,000 match the water
used in an eight-minute shower. 7 Still, billions of interactions, more complex workloads
and growth in resource-constrained regions can create meaningful cumulative impacts.
The most meaningful assessment therefore depends on workload, utilization, location,

grid mix, cooling design, local water availability and who bears the cost of supporting
infrastructure, not one universal number.
AI’s Handprint. While much remains to be seen, AI’s environmental “handprint”—the
benefits it can enable—could potentially prove larger than its direct footprint in some
areas. Across the energy system, AI can help utilities better match electricity supply
and demand, balance grids, optimize storage and reduce wasted energy. Data centers

already account for more than 30% of corporate renewable power-purchase agreements,
helping create long-dated demand for clean generation. 8 Beyond power, AI can improve
precision agriculture, streamline transportation and inventories, and support product
1 BloombergNEF , Data Centers on Track to Suck Up a Fifth of US Power Use by 2035, July 21, 2026.
2 BloombergNEF , AI Buildout Drives 76% Power Bill Jump on Largest US Grid, May 14, 2026.

Investment Implications
Responsible AI growth requires
investment in power generation,

grid modernization, energy storage,
advanced cooling, water treatment
and reuse, efficient computing, and
materials recovery and recycling.
Diversified environmental and thematic
strategies may offer exposure to
these physical enablers while reducing
reliance on benchmark names.

design, predictive maintenance, waste sorting and recycling. AI is transforming climate
risk management by using satellite imagery, weather data and predictive analytics to
forecast floods, wildfires and extreme weather events, enabling governments, companies,
and investors to anticipate risks and allocate capital more effectively (Exhibit 2). These
gains are not automatic: they depend on innovation, adoption, and whether efficiency

improvements reduce total resource use rather than simply stimulate more demand. All
industries are faced with the opportunity to responsibly use AI to make operations more
efficient, and much is yet to be discovered.

AI’s potential handprint becomes more tangible in transportation, agriculture and climate
resilience. In aviation, for example, AI can combine weather forecasts, flight paths and
atmospheric conditions to reroute aircraft around regions where warming contrails
are most likely to form—reducing climate impact without requiring a new aircraft or

fuel system. On farms, machine learning can interpret aerial imagery and field-level
sensor data to identify crop stress early, directing water, fertilizer and chemicals to
where they are needed and helping growers improve yields while reducing inputs. AI
can also strengthen climate preparedness by analyzing satellite imagery, weather data
and infrastructure exposure to detect deforestation, anticipate floods or wildfires,
and identify communities, facilities and supply chains most vulnerable to physical

hazards. These are just a few examples that illustrate how AI’s value can extend beyond
incremental efficiency gains to reshape real-world decisions.
Investment Considerations. The challenges created by AI also create potential
investment opportunities. Companies are investing in efficient chips, closed-loop cooling,
reclaimed-water systems, energy storage, grid upgrades and cleaner firm power to
reduce resource intensity and improve reliability. Professionally managed environmental

and thematic strategies can target these “picks and shovels” of the digital buildout,
alongside businesses using AI to improve efficiency across energy management,
water infrastructure, environmental services, waste management and compute. A
disciplined thematic approach can diversify exposure beyond the largest benchmark
constituents while positioning for structural growth in power demand, grid modernization
and resource efficiency. Focusing solely on AI’s footprint risks overlooking both the

companies helping it scale more efficiently and the broader environmental benefits its
handprint may enable.

THOUGHT OF THE WEEK
The Beauty of September
Ariana Chiu, Assistant Vice President and Investment Strategist
Call it an eventful first 13 trading days of September: Brent crude re-eclipsed $100 per
barrel, diesel prices surpassed $6 per gallon for the first time in history, the 10-year U.S.
Treasury yield breached 5%, 9 and the Fed joined the ECB and the BoJ in hiking rates for

the first time since July 2023. Through it all, the S&P 500 has shed 1.4% since the start
of the month, while its equal-weighted counterpart is down by 3.3%.
It’s not all too unexpected that September would be a choppy month for U.S. Equities.
Historical seasonal patterns suggest as much. Since 1928, the average S&P 500 return
in September has come out to -1.1%, 10 not only the worst month of the year on average
but also the only month when the benchmark has ended lower more often than it’s

ended higher. Similar weakness has been seen in midterm election years, with the S&P
500 producing an average September return of -0.8% since 1950. 10
The good news is that this has historically been an attractive entry point for U.S. Equity
investors. Call it the beauty of September: In contrast to September’s average decline,
average returns improve in October (+3.0%), November (+2.7%), and December (+0.8%).
Indeed, the S&P 500 has never been negative in the six or 12 months post-midterm

election since 1950 (Exhibit 4A), boasting an impressive average return of 13.8% and
15.8% over those respective periods. 10
Looking ahead to the coming weeks, we have little reason to believe that 2026
will diverge from the historical trend. Volatility is likely to rise into October before
moderating post-election, in line with what’s typical in the months surrounding the
midterms (Exhibit 4B). A quieter corporate calendar ahead of Q3 earnings means

investor focus will remain on incoming macro data, the path of energy prices, and the
Fed’s policy path.
In the meantime, while the S&P 500 still stands just 2.7% below its mid-August high,
churn beneath the surface already appears to be taking place. Just 38% of S&P 500
members now stand above their 50-day moving average versus 70% a mere month ago. 9
Far from worrisome, a correction in the near term in the context of a strong fundamental
economic backdrop would be a potential buying opportunity in our view.

in portfolios.

Securities indexes assume reinvestment of all distributions and interest payments. Indexes are unmanaged and do not take into account fees or expenses. It is not possible to
invest directly in an index. Indexes are all based in U.S. dollars.
S&P 500 Index is a stock market index tracking the stock performance of 500 leading companies listed on stock exchanges in the United States.

Investing involves risk, including the possible loss of principal. Past performance is no guarantee of future results.

or sale of any security or investment strategy. Merrill offers a broad range of brokerage, investment advisory and other services. There are important differences between brokerage and
investment advisory services, including the type of advice and assistance provided, the fees charged, and the rights and obligations of the parties. It is important to understand the differences,
particularly when determining which service or services to select. For more information about these services and their differences, speak with your Merrill financial advisor.

Investments have varying degrees of risk. Some of the risks involved with equity securities include the possibility that the value of the stocks may fluctuate in response to events specific to

the companies or markets, as well as economic, political or social events in the U.S. or abroad. Small cap and mid cap companies pose special risks, including possible illiquidity and greater price
volatility than funds consisting of larger, more established companies. Investing in fixed-income securities may involve certain risks, including the credit quality of individual issuers, possible
prepayments, market or economic developments and yields and share price fluctuations due to changes in interest rates. When interest rates go up, bond prices typically drop, and vice versa.

Investments in high-yield bonds (sometimes referred to as “junk bonds”) offer the potential for high current income and attractive total return, but involves certain risks. Changes in economic
conditions or other circumstances may adversely affect a junk bond issuer’s ability to make principal and interest payments. Income from investing in municipal bonds is generally exempt
from Federal and state taxes for residents of the issuing state. While the interest income is tax-exempt, any capital gains distributed are taxable to the investor. Income for some investors

may be subject to the Federal Alternative Minimum Tax (AMT). Treasury bills are less volatile than longer-term fixed income securities and are guaranteed as to timely payment of principal and
interest by the U.S. government. Bonds are subject to interest rate, inflation and credit risks. Investments in foreign securities (including ADRs) involve special risks, including foreign currency
risk and the possibility of substantial volatility due to adverse political, economic or other developments. These risks are magnified for investments made in emerging markets. Investments in

a certain industry or sector may pose additional risk due to lack of diversification and sector concentration. There are special risks associated with an investment in commodities, such as gold,
including market price fluctuations, regulatory changes, interest rate changes, credit risk, economic changes and the impact of adverse political or financial factors.
Alternative investments are speculative and involve a high degree of risk.
Alternative investments are intended for qualified investors only. Alternative Investments such as derivatives, hedge funds, private credit, private equity funds, and funds of funds can result

in higher return potential but also higher loss potential. Changes in economic conditions or other circumstances may adversely affect your investments. Before you invest in alternative
investments, you should consider your overall financial situation, how much money you have to invest, your need for liquidity, and your tolerance for risk.

## Document 351: Eurozone _ Growth on track despite the turbulence (2026-09-24)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Eurozone _ Growth on track despite the turbulence (2026-09-24).pdf
Report source: BNP Paribas
Date: 2026-09-24
Pages: 2
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

8
economic-research.bnpparibas.comEcoPerspectives / 3rd Quarter 2026 / September 2026
The bank
for a changing
world
GROWTH ON TRACK DESPITE THE TURBULENCE
Growth was stronger than expected in Q2 and is set to strengthen further in the coming quarters. According to our fore-
casts, it will reach 1.5% in 2027 after 1.1% in 2026. This growth is expected to be driven by investment and its knock-on
effect on exports. On an annual average basis, inflation is expected to stand at 3% in 2026 and 2.8% in 2027, peaking at

the end of 2026. The ECB is expected to continue its monetary tightening. At this stage, a single further rate rise, aimed at
keeping inflation expectations anchored, is expected in December. The impact of rising interest rates (key policy rates and
market rates) on public finances and private sector borrowing conditions will be worth monitoring.
GROWTH: BEYOND THE SHOCKS, A RESILIENT MOMENTUM
Growth in the Eurozone remained robust in the first half of 2026, at

0.25% q/q in Q1 and 0.33% q/q in Q2, excluding Ireland (where economic
activity is volatile). It was driven by Germany, where signs of a recovery
are becoming clearer (see Germany). Our baseline scenario forecasts
growth of 1.1% in 2026, followed by 1.5% in 2027 on an annual average
basis. This growth is expected to be driven by the strengthening of
trends already underway in public and private investment (particularly
in defence and AI) and in exports (trade in services and intra-zone trade

in goods). Household consumption is likely to be resilient too. According
to our forecasts, job creation would continue to underpin household
incomes, thereby cushioning the impact of inflation.
A NEW LEASE OF LIFE FOR INDUSTRY
The Eurozone is undergoing one of the most profound industrial trans-
formations in its history. This is giving rise to divergences between key
sectors but, above all, to new drivers of growth whose effects are beco-
ming increasingly apparent (see our analysis). Indeed, the signs of an

economic recovery are growing stronger. Business sentiment indicators
show a marked improvement in the economic outlook across most sec-
tors (Chart 2), driven primarily by electronics (AI, digitalisation), the
automotive sector and aerospace. European industry is thus benefi -
ting from strong demand for capital goods; a structural trend that will
continue to be fuelled by ongoing investment cycles in AI, defence and
electrification. The introduction of minimum European content quotas

in the draft law on the industrial accelerator, together with the Com -
mission’s intention to tighten the criteria for access to European public
procurement markets, will provide additional support for European in-
dustry in the longer term.
FOREIGN TRADE: A REPOSITIONING THAT IS PAYING OFF
Following a decline in 2026, linked to rising energy import costs, the
trade surplus is expected to rise to around 3.0% of GDP in 2027 (of
which 2% of GDP for goods and 1% for services). Growth in European ex-

ports has slowed compared with its pre-Covid trend and is being ham-
pered by the decline in Chinese and US markets. However, Eurozone
exports in volume rose significantly in Q2 (+3.4% q/q).
More importantly, since Liberation Day (April 2025), Eurozone coun -
tries have found significant sources of growth amongst other European
partners, which has enabled them to cushion the decline in exports to
certain third markets. This is evident in exports to the rest of the EU,

EFTA countries (Switzerland in particular) and, to a lesser extent, the
United Kingdom, Ukraine and the Balkans (Chart 3). The momentum of
intra-European trade therefore remains very strong. It is expected to
strengthen further with the anticipated investments in defence, AI and
electrification.
FINANCING: FINANCIAL CONDITIONS ARE EXPECTED TO REMAIN TIGHT
Financial conditions in the Eurozone remained tight in Q2 2026, accor-
ding to indices calculated by the ECB (including the BIG [Broad Inter -

mediation Gauge]). They are expected to tighten more significantly in
the second half of the year: i/ the rise in long-term bond yields, which
has accelerated since the outbreak of the conflict in Iran, is expected to
continue; and ii/ the ECB will continue its monetary tightening (+25 bps

EcoPerspectives / 3rd Quarter 2026 / September 2026 economic-research.bnpparibas.com
The bank
for a changing
world
in Q3, then in Q4). For the time being, the debt burden is still weighing
only moderately on public finances due to the long average maturity of
countries’ debt (8.2 years on average in the Eurozone in July), but this
burden will increase by 2030.
On the corporate front, the rise in interest rates has remained contained
(+2 bps since January 2026, reaching 3.9% in July), which has contri -

buted to the strong growth in new investment loans (cumulated over
one year, +9.1% y/y in July compared with 15.3% y/y in September 2025).
By contrast, cumulative net debt securities issues over one year re -
mained stable in July, whilst cumulative net issues of listed shares over
one year halved month on month.
Rising interest rates will continue to hamper new variable-rate
home loans to households (around 65% of new lending) more signi -
ficantly than fixed-rate loans, for which rates stood in July at 3.69%

(+4 bps m/m) and 3.36% (+2 bps m/m) respectively. New home loans,
which are stable in 2026 on a cumulative over one year basis, are there-
fore unlikely to see any improvement. A fall in real-estate transactions
could weigh on new consumer credit through a reduction in expenditure
on home furnishings and renovations, whereas consumer credit has
been fairly buoyant so far (year-to-date, +5.2% y/y in July compared
with +1.4% y/y in January).
LABOUR MARKET: RESILIENCE YET TO BE CONFIRMED

Employment in the Eurozone rose again by an average of 0.1% q/q in
the first half of 2026, driven by Spain and Portugal. Given the growth
outlook in these two countries, this momentum is likely to continue,
whilst business sentiment surveys in Germany suggest that employ -
ment is beginning to stabilise. Nevertheless, employment levels are
plateauing (France, the Netherlands) or even falling in other countries
(Austria, Finland), which is likely to slow the decline in the Eurozone

unemployment rate, meaning it is expected to remain close to current
levels in 2027. Furthermore, second-round effects of the rise in inflation
on wages cannot be ruled out: the ECB’s wage tracker suggests wage
growth of close to 3% by summer 2027 (2.9% y/y in June 2027, compared
with 2.6% in September).
INFLATION: A PEAK EXPECTED IN LATE 2026
Changes in wage dynamics will be a key factor in the inflation out -
look for 2027, whilst energy price rises are picking up again, driven

by the conflict in the Middle East. This rise is even sharper than in
the spring for gas (linked to restocking ahead of winter) and is likely
to feed through to electricity prices in several countries. We therefore
anticipate inflation peaking at 3.7% y/y in Q4 2026, before easing as the
energy component falls. On an annual average, headline inflation is
expected to stand at 3% in 2026 and then at 2.8% in 2027.
At this stage, the pass-through of rising energy prices to other com -

ponents of the consumer price index, particularly services, remains li -
mited. The alternative core indicators monitored by the ECB, including
the PCCI (Persistent and Common Component of Inflation, 2.1% in July),
remain close to the 2% target. Core inflation, however, is expected to
peak two months later than headline inflation (3% in March 2027).
Inflationary risks are nevertheless tilted to the upside. These are lin -
ked to food prices, which are expected to be affected by the energy

shock and weather conditions (summer drought, El Niño); they could
also stem from a delayed impact of energy price rises on underlying
inflation. The rise in prices of energy-dependent inputs (notably che -
micals and plastics) and those linked to the AI sector is expected to be
sustained. Growth, which we expect to remain robust over the coming
quarters, will also fuel underlying inflationary pressures.
MONETARY POLICY: TIGHTENING WITHOUT SLOWING (GROWTH)

Following two 25-basis-point rises (in June and September), we ex -
pect a third in December, which would bring the deposit facility rate
to 2.75%. It would then be in slightly restrictive territory (we estimate
the upper bound of the neutral rate to be 2.5%). This rise would be
aimed primarily at anchoring inflation expectations and, consequently,
at curbing the pass-through effects of inflation. Monetary tightening
would therefore remain limited. Indeed, the overshoot of the inflation

target would be temporary, and the operational framework adopted by
the ECB in June 2025 allows it to tolerate this, provided it remains brief.
However, it cannot be ruled out that the ECB might take further action
if the decline in inflation proves slower than expected.
PUBLIC FINANCES ARE CONTINGENT ON RISING INTEREST RATES
The fiscal impulse in the Eurozone is expected to be clearly positive in
2026 due to the rise in defence and infrastructure spending in Germany.

This will contribute to a widening of the country’s primary deficit (from
+0.9 pp of GDP to 2.8%, according to our forecasts). The improvement in
public finances is expected to stall in both France and Italy, at least in
2026, due to the impact of inflation on the debt burden via index-linked
bonds. This trend is expected to reverse in 2027 with less expansionary
fiscal policies. Nevertheless, the increase in the debt service burden
would fuel a further rise in the public deficit, which would thus reach

around 3.3% and 3.5% of GDP in 2026 and 2027 respectively (compared
with 2.9% in 2025). Against this backdrop, the Eurozone’s public debt-
to-GDP ratio is projected to exceed 90% of GDP in 2026 and reach 93%
of GDP in 2027, according to our forecasts.
Article completed on 15 September 2026

► 今年以來適 逢美伊衝突難題遲遲未解 ，高油
價讓聯準會重啟升息且堆高升息 預期，面對
部分聯準會官員保持觀望立場 ，利率期貨顯
示聯準會將升息一碼 且時點可能在 12月，未
來1年則存在3碼潛在升息空間 ，考量地緣衝
突短期 內持 續 ， 若 通膨難 以改善 至政 策目
標，預期聯準會2027年將維持緊縮貨幣政策
的鷹派立場來對抗通膨，然美國2年期公債利
率已多反映 未來升息預期 ，後續仍視美國核
心物價發展，引導短天期公債利率走向。
► 儘管全球利率大幅上升 ，然對於整體 金融環
境影響 甚微，反映美 股和信用 市場低波動狀
態，體現實質 政策利率低於 緊縮利率 區間與
彭博金 融情勢指數變化不大 ，當前美國貨幣
政策與市場流動性仍偏寬鬆 ，倘若地緣擾動

持續造成通 膨居高難下 ，恐進一步推升聯準
會升息預期 ，疊加政府財政 風險與貨幣政策
透明度降 ，加劇長 天期公債利率 上行風險 ，
長天期債以交易為主，不建議長期持有。

► 特斯拉3Q26財報好壞參半 — 毛利率與交付
量超預 期 ， 營收 大 致符合 ，營業 利潤 遜預
期，最大意外是自由現金流達 15億美元，高
於共識負43億，資本開支指引下調成憂慮。
► 早前公司展示下代 Roadster能力，本質上是
光環車 ，定價高受眾少 ，2028 年才有望投
產，盈利貢獻有限 。Model 3/Y成為大眾商
品，競爭激烈 ，利潤率壓縮 。正如傳統品牌
以賽道或參賽展示技術 ，「飛天」旨在展示
獨有跨界整合能力，維持品牌形象溢價。
► 至於和SpaceX的潛在合併，核心在於貫通多
頂 業 務， 提 高 資 本 運 用 效 率， 共同分擔
Terafab 的開支等 ，現時兩家合計市值約為
3.5兆美元。這項巨型潛在交易 ，除了要跨過
極高表決門檻外，SpaceX具極敏感的美國國
防及NASA合作背景 ，而特斯拉有超過 20%
業務倚重上海超級工廠 ，合併存在嚴重地緣
衝突。預期馬斯克將在川普離任前 ，加速執
行Tesla與SpaceX 合併。

投資焦點
► 今年9月適逢地緣擾動持續推升高油價、聯準
會重啟 升息 、財政部 與企 業發債 浪潮 等 影
響，形塑債市今年以來單月最差表現 。檢視
2021年以來美國投資級債前十大單月最差總
報酬，2022年聯準會 3月啟動升息年度就佔
一半以上，顯示利率風險仍為近5年來債市最
大風險 ，然檢視單月最差報酬表現未來期間
總報酬，除2022年1月和3月聯準會重啟升息
前夕與當月表現明顯較弱，多數於未來1年後
多呈現正報酬 ，反映隨利率走升放大孳息效
益，同時挹注債券長期持有之報酬空間。
► 今年來債券市場利率波動明顯推升信用債利
差，其 中僅 以低 信 評債利 差升幅 較顯 ，此
外，9月以來利率連動度高及AI相關產業債種
也成為利差擴大重災區 ，凸顯債市投資人減
輕市場不確定性的投資傾向 ，考量信用債市
歷經利率上行利空洗禮 ，信用利差同步擴大

創造收益空間 ，可把握債信體質仍佳企業債
鎖利良機，並透過時間複利力量強化收益。
利率波動升，僅低信評利差擴大程度相對明顯9月為美國投資級債今年最差單月報酬
用時間換空間，單月最差表現形塑未來報酬機會

### Page 8

► 受聯準會重啟升息影響 ，過去一年美國公債
殖利率曲線持續陡峭化 ，其中利率最大升幅
落在短天期的 2至3年期間，美國核心通膨與
就業仍具韌性 ，聯準會將持續保持 緊縮貨幣
政策步調來應對高通膨 ，考量美國高物價與
高利率環境短期 難消，宜透過縮短債券配置
天期並進行動態調整 ，為因應後續利率不確
定性環境的良策。
► 為因應利率不確定性 ，投資人可採取債券梯
的投資配置 ，主要選取不同到期期間及不同
類型債劵進行 組合，債券梯投資重點主要在
於，根據殖利率曲線型態及利率動態建立不
同期間 債券梯，假設市場利率走升且殖利率
曲線正斜率 ，建立短天期債券梯除了降低利
率風險 ，有 短期 閒 置資金 及孳息 需求 投資
人，亦可取得穩定孳息 收益。若後續投資人
有短期資金需求 ，可以視情況將短期債券到
期後資金回收 ，降低只投資單一債券須持至
到期或直接變現的流動性風險。
投資焦點
美國公債殖利率曲線持續為正斜率
高利率波動持續，用策略迎戰市場擾動
資料來源：Bloomberg，凱基證券整理

建立短天期債券梯，分散風險及穩定現金流
債券梯
經過第2年第一檔 債券到期，視資金需求 及市況與利率 動
態決定後續配置

短天期債券梯 ：假設買入 2檔各 3、4、5及7年到期債券 ，
聚焦 短期穩定收益
► 債券梯是一種分散投資方式 ，主要選取不同到期期間及不同
類型債劵進行組合，可兼具：
1) 分散利率風險： 將資金分別買進不同到期年期的債券 ，以分
散利率風險。
2) 分散信用風險： 分散投資不同發行 機構債券，不會重壓 單一
債券並可分散信用違約風險。
3) 降低流動性風險： 若投資人有短期資金需求 ，可以視情況將
短期債券到期後資金回收 ，降低只投資單一債券須持至到期
或直接變現之流動性風險。

### Page 9

9
股市
 美股第三季獲利年增率預估為雙位數成長 ，市場靜待財報結果與展望 ，和對AI資本支出回收影響 。
考量地緣衝突 、殖利率偏高、評價面壓力與鄰近美國期中選舉 ，短期金融市場紛擾不止 ，可增加防
禦股增強耐震度 。長期AI、科技發展趨勢不變 ，逢股市拉回時可分批布局 ，分散配置於不同國家與
產業，降低市場整體波動，亦可以槓鈴策略(防禦+成長)穩中求利。
 歐、日股可作為分散配置重點 ，全球利率走揚有利銀行股 。此外，地緣衝突頻傳 ，歐洲國防軍工仍
有投資題材。
策略風格：可增加防禦股配置，中長期看
好AI題材如科技及半導體，非AI部分關注
醫療保健生技產業
區域：日本銀行股、歐洲國防
債市
 全球公債殖利率攀升 ，預估今年底前主要國家仍有升息空間 ，殖利率偏高環境 使中短天期債券提供
吸引力，逢利率走高、鎖利領息。高信評債體質較佳 ，具穩定現金流與還款能力 ，聚焦風險調整後
利差具吸引力者，包含金融、通訊及公用事業等產業。
債種：中短天期高信評債鎖利，產業如金
融、通訊及公用事業等

匯市
 歐洲政治風險上升、歐元短期弱盤，美元高檔有撐。
 日本銀行再度升息時間不定，日圓區間震盪。
美元高檔有撐
商品
 中東地緣衝突短期難解，油價維持高位但震盪加劇。
 短期美國雙率走高 ，使金價短期承壓 ，惟美歐日財政赤字壓力與去美元化 ，使全球央行與長期投資
人買盤依舊，提供黃金中長期穩定支撐。
短期黃金有壓，中長期維持偏多看法
類別 市場看法 偏好資產
